### [1] [CoVLM-Bench: A Real-World Benchmark for Cooperative Driving Question Answering and Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35823)
*Kang Yang,Shuai Liu,Hang Li,Yance Fang,Deying Li,Yongcai Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CoVLM-Bench基准和CoVLM-Drive模型，用于在车路协同场景下联合评估视觉语言模型的问答与轨迹规划能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型（VLMs）多聚焦于自车视角，而基础设施侧观测提供了超视距信息。传统系统将此类信息转化为几何表示，直接将其融入VLMs有助于提升协同场景理解与规划，但此前缺乏在同一真实车路场景下对问答和规划的联合评估。

Method: 构建CoVLM-Bench基准，包含2,196对帧、35,136个协同驾驶问答（CDQA）标注及基于记录 ego 运动的未来轨迹目标；开发CoVLM-Drive统一VLM基线，直接利用配对视图进行CDQA和协同规划（CP）。

Result: 实验表明，CDQA适配提高了答案准确率；CoVLM-Drive在最终位置误差（FDE）上优于对比的V2X规划器；问答初始化和推理监督均降低了规划误差。

Conclusion: CoVLM-Bench和CoVLM-Drive为训练和比较用于协同场景理解和规划的VLMs提供了支持，证明了直接集成基础设施视角的有效性。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have made substantial progress in autonomous driving, but their success has primarily been studied in ego-centric scenes. Infrastructure-side observations provide views beyond the ego vehicle's field of view, yet conventional cooperative-driving systems typically transform them into geometric representations for downstream perception and planning. Directly incorporating these views into VLMs offers an opportunity to improve cooperative scene understanding and trajectory planning. However, question answering and trajectory planning have not been jointly evaluated on the same real-world vehicle-infrastructure scenes. We present CoVLM-Bench, a benchmark for cooperative driving question answering (CDQA) and cooperative planning (CP) on vehicle-infrastructure paired scenes. CoVLM-Bench provides scene-grounded CDQA annotations, three-part rationales as auxiliary supervision, and future trajectory targets derived from recorded ego motion. It contains 2,196 paired frames with 35,136 CDQA annotations, while CP predicts six waypoints over a three-second horizon. The annotations combine model-assisted drafting, record-based computation, and human verification. Built upon CoVLM-Bench, we introduce CoVLM-Drive, a unified VLM baseline that directly uses paired views for both CDQA and CP. Experiments show that CDQA adaptation improves answer accuracy and that CoVLM-Drive reaches a lower FDE than the compared V2X planners; QA initialization and rationale supervision each reduce planning error. Together, CoVLM-Bench and CoVLM-Drive support the training and comparison of VLMs for cooperative scene understanding and planning.

</details>


### [2] [HERO: Histology Encoder for Robust Representation in Oncology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35943)
*Zhi Li,Eghbal Amidi,Yating Cheng,Tyson Dawson,Gorkem Can Ates,Shuzhen Kuang,Norsang Lama,Md Ashequr Rahman,Zhiying Lu,Elisabeth K. Kong,Milan Radovich,David Spetzler,Matthew Oberley,George W. Sledge,Ming Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HERO模型，一种针对病理图像的基础模型，通过形态学平衡语料库和高精度锚定技术，在保持高性能的同时显著提升了对不同医院、扫描仪和染色协议的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有病理基础模型虽在标准基准测试中表现优异，但往往将采集因素（如中心、扫描仪、染色协议）与生物学信息混合嵌入，导致在面对训练数据外的临床实际场景时产生误差，阻碍了安全临床应用。亟需开发既具备高质量表征能力又对采集偏移具有强鲁棒性的模型。

Method: 构建ViT-G/14架构的病理基础模型HERO；使用DINO和iBOT目标进行预训练；引入高分辨率Gram锚定技术进行优化；基于约57.5万张全切片图像中的5亿个形态学平衡图块进行训练。

Result: HERO在公共基准测试中对中心、扫描仪和染色变化展现出最强的鲁棒性；在图块级分类、分割和基因表达预测任务中性能相当；在39项评估的幻灯片级临床任务中平均排名第一；在六个基准框架的平均加权分析中表现最佳。

Conclusion: HERO模型证明了在大规模病理基础模型训练中兼顾表征质量与采集鲁棒性的可行性，为计算病理学的临床安全部署提供了更可靠的技术支持。

Abstract: Foundation models trained on large pathology image corpora now provide strong, transferable representations for computational pathology. Over the past few years a series of such models has been released, each trained on more slides than the last; on standard classification and segmentation benchmarks, the leading models are now separated by small margins. In clinical use, however, the foundation model is applied to images from hospitals, scanners, and staining protocols outside its training data. Encoders generally embed these acquisition factors alongside biological information, which may introduce downstream errors and hinder safe clinical adoption. A pathology foundation model should therefore be robust to acquisition shift without giving up representation quality, yet robustness is seldom the axis along which models are compared. In this report, we introduce HERO (Histology Encoder for Robust Representation in Oncology), a ViT-G/14 pathology foundation model trained with the DINO and iBOT objectives and refined with high-resolution Gram anchoring on a morphology-balanced corpus of 500 million tiles from approximately 575,000 clinical whole-slide images. Across the evaluated public benchmarks, HERO shows the strongest robustness to center, scanner, and stain variation among the compared state-of-the-art foundation models, performs comparably on tile-level classification, segmentation, and gene-expression prediction, ranks first on average across 39 evaluated slide-level clinical tasks, and, under an equal-weighted framework-level analysis, has the best average rank across the six benchmark frameworks.

</details>


### [3] [HEIR: Learning Human-Entity Interactions with Functional Roles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35955)
*Di Wen,Wenhao Guo,Yuedong Tan,Yun Huang,Minheng Wu,Zhihang Chen,Haiwen Sun,Fei Teng,Zhiyuan Gao,Yufeng Zhang,Yuanhao Luo,Jingqi Zhang,Yufan Chen,Junwei Zheng,Ruiping Liu,Jiale Wei,Kailun Yang,Kunyu Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HEIR基准和CoRISP模型，通过联合评估个体关系与完整事件结构，解决人类-实体交互（HOI）中事件组成测量不足的问题，显著提升多角色/共享参与者场景下的预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HOI指标仅评分个体链接，未能充分衡量完整的事件构成；需明确谁对哪些实体采取行动及其角色，以支持具身智能体在共享环境中的预期与协调。

Method: 构建包含18,730张图像的HEIR基准数据集，涵盖对象、人际和自我导向交互；提出CoRISP模型，利用共享实体身份组合角色条件证据，通过基数和角色多重性势函数耦合分配，实现精确的每事件归一化预测。

Result: CoRISP在HEIR重复角色事件和共享参与者图像上分别提升2.87和3.82 Set mAP；在V-COCO上达到73.72/76.23角色AP和61.06/68.59完整集AP。研究表明联合学习事件组成与个体关系具有重要价值。

Conclusion: 完整的事件结构评估对于理解人类-实体交互至关重要，CoRISP通过结构化预测有效提升了复杂交互场景的性能，代码与数据集已公开。

Abstract: Understanding human-entity interactions requires recovering each person-action event's participants, roles, and shared identities. This structure can support embodied agents by clarifying who acts on which entities and how, informing anticipation and coordination in shared environments. Standard HOI metrics score individual links, leaving complete event composition undermeasured. We introduce HEIR (Human-Entity Interactions with Functional Roles), an image benchmark for complete grounded participant-role sets across object, interpersonal, and self-directed interactions. It contains 18,730 images, six roles, 105 actions, and 437 nouns, with shared entities, role changes, and repeated fillers; 51.6% of images contain multiple actors and 62.1% contain multiple actions. HEIR pairs relation AP with complete-set AP and structural evaluation. We also introduce CoRISP (Compositional Role-aware Interaction Set Prediction), which uses shared entity identities to combine role-conditioned evidence and predict normalized participant-role sets. Cardinality and role-multiplicity potentials couple assignments through event size and role composition, with exact per-event normalization. Across 16 baselines, relation and complete-event rankings diverge even after aligning action weights. CoRISP leads the evaluated systems on repeated-role events and shared-participant images in HEIR by 2.87 and 3.82 Set mAP points, respectively. On V-COCO, CoRISP achieves 73.72/76.23 role AP and 61.06/68.59 complete-set AP on two-slot actions under Scenarios 1/2. These results show the value of learning and evaluating event composition alongside individual relations. The code and dataset are publicly available at https://github.com/Kratos-Wen/HEIR.

</details>


### [4] [Systematic Multi-Agent Vision-and-Language Navigation: Formulation, Benchmark, and Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35965)
*Yunzhe Xu,Zhe Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出首个系统化多智能体视觉-语言导航（MAVLN）框架，通过11,724个测试集实例和TRISS系统解决多机器人协作中的任务依赖与资源冲突问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLN研究主要关注单智能体跟随单一指令，但现实应用需要多机器人团队协同完成超出个体能力的复杂任务。

Method: 将多智能体VLN形式化为带约束的协调问题（含子任务依赖、存在锁及持有链），构建包含11,724个片段、145个场景的MAVLN数据集；提出TRISS系统，集成LLM子任务调度器、共享拓扑记忆及冲突感知执行机制。

Result: 建立TRISS作为全面基线，实验揭示在调度、规划和执行层面存在显著改进空间，证实了MAVLN任务约束下协调的高难度。

Conclusion: 多智能体视觉-语言导航面临复杂的协调挑战，需进一步突破调度、规划与执行机制以应对任务约束。

Abstract: Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) has largely focused on a single agent following a single instruction, yet many real-world applications require teams of robots to tackle tasks beyond the capabilities of any individual agent. We present Systematic Multi-Agent Vision-and-Language Navigation, providing, to our knowledge, the first systematic formalization of multi-agent VLN as a constrained coordination problem: each mission consists of subtasks carrying dependency and resource constraints (presence locks and holding chains). A verified four-stage crafting pipeline instantiates the task as MAVLN, comprising 11,724 episodes across 145 scenes with teams of up to four agents under three instruction regimes, accompanied by tailored constraint-aware metrics. We further present TRISS, a coordination-ready navigation system coupling an LLM-based subtask scheduler, a shared topological memory that turns each agent's exploration into team knowledge, and a conflict-aware execution mechanism that realizes simultaneous intentions as collision-free routes. Extensive experiments establish TRISS as a comprehensive baseline and reveal substantial room for improvement across scheduling, planning, and execution, highlighting the challenges of coordinating under MAVLN task constraints. Project page: https://xyz9911.github.io/mavln.

</details>


### [5] [Persistence Forcing: Exploiting Feature Specialization in Pixel-Space Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36014)
*Chong Wang,Zixuan Fu,Shiqi Huang,Siyuan Yang,Hao Cheng,Bihan Wen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种在像素空间扩散Transformer（DiT）中引入异质细化（heterogeneous refinement）的新方法，将特征分为持久特征（persistent features）和活动特征（active features），并设计了Persistence Forcing (PerF) 机制，利用持久特征引导活动特征的细化过程，从而提升图像生成的全局一致性和细节质量。该方法在ImageNet数据集上取得了接近或超越现有先进模型的性能，同时参数量更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的像素空间扩散Transformer（DiTs）对隐藏表示进行均匀细化，但自然图像在不同粒度层级上具有不同的结构特性：全局结构可以紧凑表示，而局部纹理和细节需要更丰富的表示。因此，需要一种能够适应这种多粒度特性的细化策略。

Method: 1. 引入异质细化（Heterogeneous Refinement）：在深度方向上为不同特征组分配不同的细化预算，形成稀疏细化的持久特征（编码全局结构）和频繁细化的活动特征（编码高频细节）。
2. 提出 Persistence Forcing (PerF) 机制：显式利用持久-活动特征的组织结构，让持久特征持续条件化活动特征，使稳定的全局信息指导更精细视觉细节的生成。
3. 在生成采样过程中，这种交互产生有意义的引导方向，促进全局结构的连贯性，并与无分类器引导（classifier-free guidance）互补。

Result: 在ImageNet 256×256数据集上，PerF-L模型达到FID 1.91，仅用一半参数量即接近JiT-H模型的1.86；PerF-H模型在ImageNet 256×256和512×512数据集上分别取得FID 1.63和1.76的优异性能。

Conclusion: 通过异质细化和持久-活动特征引导机制，有效提升了像素空间扩散模型的生成质量，实现了更高效、更高质量的图像生成，为后续研究提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Pixel-space diffusion Transformers (DiTs) directly operate on high-dimensional visual data, yet their hidden representations typically undergo uniform refinement across depth. Natural images, however, are inherently organized at different levels of granularity. Global structure can often be represented compactly, whereas local textures and fine details require richer representations. Motivated by this, we introduce heterogeneous refinement in pixel-space DiTs, assigning different feature groups distinct refinement budgets across depth. Consequently, an ordered feature specialization emerges: sparsely refined features predominantly encode global visual structure, whereas more frequently refined features increasingly specialize toward localized, high-frequency details. We refer to these two groups as persistent and active features, respectively. Building on this emergent specialization, we introduce Persistence Forcing (PerF), which explicitly exploits this persistent--active feature organization for pixel-space image generation. This enables persistent features to continuously condition actively refined features, allowing stable global information to guide the ongoing refinement of finer visual details. During generative sampling, this interaction further induces a meaningful guidance direction that promotes coherent global structure and naturally complements classifier-free guidance. On ImageNet $256\times256$, PerF-L achieves FID of $1.91$, approaching $1.86$ of JiT-H with only half the parameters, while PerF-H further achieves FID of $1.63$ and $1.76$ on ImageNet $256\times256$ and $512\times512$, respectively.

</details>


### [6] [CoDimRecon: Agentic Reconstruction of Sim-Ready 3D Scenes with Deformable Curves, Surfaces, and Volumes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36024)
*Shuzhao Xie,Lelin Wang,Guying Lin,Zhi Wang,Minchen Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CoDimRecon框架，通过多视角RGB重建包含刚性、可动及变形物体的仿真就绪3D场景，解决现有方法难以处理变形体的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D场景重建方法主要假设物体为刚性，缺乏对变形体（deformables）的有效支持，而变形体的几何表示依赖维度且行为模型超越弹性范畴。

Method: 采用智能体驱动框架：利用场景级几何先验确定尺度与布局，生成网格引导细节；分解可动物体为带关节的部件；针对变形体按类别重建曲线（中心线+半径）、曲面（壳层+厚度）和体积（水密实体）；复用模拟器技能初始化物理参数，并通过行为测试触发针对性修正。

Result: 在Replica和ScanNet++数据集上实现具有竞争力的组合重建精度，成功生成杆状、壳状和固体变形资产，并验证了机器人在三种表示形式下的交互能力，如纸张折叠中的塑性弯曲模拟。

Conclusion: CoDimRecon填补了非刚性物体重建空白，通过多维几何表示和行为反馈机制，实现了从观测到仿真就绪资产的端到端自动化重建。

Abstract: Reconstructing simulation-ready 3D scenes from real-world observations enables robotics, gaming, and immersive applications, yet existing methods largely assume rigid objects. This leaves an important gap for deformables, whose simulation-ready geometry depends on dimensionality (curves, surfaces, or volumes) and whose behavior may require models beyond elasticity. We present CoDimRecon, an agentic framework that reconstructs editable scenes containing rigid, articulated, and deformable objects from multi-view RGB observations. Scene-level geometric priors ground scale and layout, while object-level generated meshes guide the agent toward detailed, compact geometry; articulated rigid objects are decomposed into movable parts with explicit joints. For deformables, category-wise agent sessions reconstruct curves as centerlines with radii, surfaces as manifold shells with thickness, and volumes as watertight solids for volumetric meshing. Reusable simulator skills initialize compatible physical models and parameters, while agent-guided behavioral tests expose mismatches and trigger targeted revisions of motion, geometry, numerics, or material modeling. On evaluated Replica and ScanNet++ scenes, CoDimRecon achieves competitive compositional reconstruction accuracy while additionally producing deformable assets for rod, shell, and solid simulation. We further demonstrate robot interactions across all three representations, including a controlled paper-folding case in which behavioral testing motivates plastic bending.

</details>


### [7] [AerialDojo-200K: A Large-Scale Benchmark Suite for Open-World Aerial Object-Goal Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36066)
*Tongtong Feng,Xin Wang,Haoran Hou,Ren Wang,Weiran Wang,Shaokai Zhu,Ziqi Jia,Hao Wang,Yu-Wei Zhan,Zongyuan Wu,Jinghao Cui,Wenwu Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AerialDojo-200K基准，包含3倍场景和18.7倍任务实例，解决现有小规模基准限制，推动开放世界无人机目标搜索研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放世界无人机目标搜索研究处于初级阶段，依赖小规模、特定环境的基准测试，存在动作空间异构和数据格式不统一的问题，阻碍了大规模训练和跨基准评估，限制了智能体的可扩展性和泛化能力。

Method: 构建包含42个仿真场景（涵盖城市、自然、基础设施、灾害四类）的大型基准套件；人工标注地标、目标对象及锚点；生成205,732个任务实例（含语义目标和图像目标），提供无碰撞参考轨迹和多视角视频；开发统一评估框架，划分分布内/外场景。

Result: 基准套件规模远超现有最大基准；对5个开源和4个闭源多模态大语言模型进行评估，发现当前模型在实现通用无人机智能体方面仍有显著差距。

Conclusion: AerialDojo-200K为开放世界无人机目标搜索提供了大规模、高质量、标准化的评估平台，揭示了当前多模态大模型在该任务中的不足，为未来通用无人机智能体研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Open-world aerial object-goal search is a foundational yet challenging task, requiring aerial agents to autonomously explore large-scale, unstructured three-dimensional environments and reach target objects specified by semantic descriptions or reference images, rather than following route-specific instructions. However, research in this task remains at a nascent stage and relies on small, environment-specific benchmarks with heterogeneous action spaces and data formats. These limitations hinder large-scale training and cross-benchmark evaluation, constraining the scalability and generalizability of aerial agents. To address this problem, we propose AerialDojo-200K, a large-scale benchmark suite for open-world aerial object-goal search, with 3 times as many scenes and 18.7 times as many task instances as the largest existing benchmark for this task. Specifically, we construct 42 simulation scenes spanning four scene families and 21 scene types, including 18 urban, 12 natural, six infrastructure, and six disaster scenes. To ensure data quality, 12 annotators spent two months manually annotating 109 landmarks, 2099 target objects, and 2099 object anchors across these scenes. We further construct 205,732 task instances, comprising over 100K semantic-goal and over 100K image-goal instances across Base, Standard, and Long-Horizon settings. Each task instance includes a collision-free reference trajectory and corresponding multi-view video recordings. We also develop a unified evaluation framework with a scene partition comprising 21 in-distribution scenes and 21 out-of-distribution scenes. Finally, our evaluation of five open-source and four closed-source multimodal large language models reveals that there is still a long way to go toward achieving general-purpose aerial agents. All can be found at https://fengtt42.github.io/AerialDojo/.

</details>


### [8] [One Geometry, Different Outcomes: Readout-Dependent Effects of the Modality Gap in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36101)
*Aditya Sharma,Divya Saxena*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过几何解释统一说明了对比视觉-语言模型中“模态间隙”（modality gap）修改对下游任务的不同影响：零样本分类受益于固定偏移，检索任务中去除间隙方向会因丢弃范数信息导致排序失真，而在混合模态检索中去除间隙可提升跨模态排名。核心发现是图像-文本均值分离近似秩一结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有研究中关于修改模态间隙效果矛盾的问题：减少间隙可提升零样本分类和跨模态对齐，但移除间隙相关结构可能损害图像-文本检索性能。需要统一解释这些任务依赖效应。

Method: 基于CLIP和SigLIP编码器，分析图像-文本嵌入空间的几何结构；发现单一主导方向捕获94.4-99.9%的平方范数，证明均值分离近似秩一；通过分解相似度得分识别三个任务特定角色，并评估不同干预策略（如投影、重归一化、指数调整）的影响。

Result: 1) 零样本分类中，查询侧固定间隙偏移减法等价于加性类别偏差；2) 标准跨模态检索中，投影出间隙方向并重新归一化残差会丢弃候选特定范数信息，引发乘性排序失真，但几何推导指数可跟踪最优网格搜索值（Spearman rho = 0.93）并在部分设置中恢复性能；3) 混合模态检索中，间隙方向按模态排序候选者，其移除可改善跨模态排名，优于随机或非间隙控制；4) 剩余语义结构定义了秩一解释的边界。

Conclusion: 该几何框架解释了为何间隙修改在不同下游任务中产生改善、退化或恢复效果，为在基于相似度的视觉-语言系统中选择间隙干预提供了原则性基础，明确了何时及为何修改间隙会改变模型行为。

Abstract: Contrastive vision-language models learn shared embedding spaces by aligning matched image-text pairs, yet their representations remain separated by a modality gap. Prior work reports divergent effects of modifying this gap: reducing it can improve zero-shot classification and cross-modal alignment, whereas removing gap-related structure can degrade image-text retrieval. In this paper, we provide a unified geometric explanation for these task-dependent effects. Across CLIP and SigLIP encoders, we find that a single dominant direction captures 94.4-99.9% of the squared norm of the image-text mean separation, revealing that the mean-separation component is approximately rank-one. A decomposition of the similarity score then identifies three task-specific roles. In zero-shot classification, query-side fixed gap-offset subtraction is exactly equivalent to an additive class bias. In standard cross-modal retrieval, projecting out the gap direction and renormalising residuals discards candidate-specific norm information, inducing a multiplicative ranking distortion; a geometry-derived exponent tracks the grid-search optimum (Spearman rho = 0.93) and restores performance in some settings, although the gains transfer unevenly. In mixed-modal retrieval, the gap direction sorts candidates by modality; its removal can improve cross-modal ranking, unlike random or non-gap controls. Residual semantic structure after removal defines the limits of the rank-one account. Together, these results explain why gap modification can improve, degrade, or restore performance across downstream settings. By clarifying when and why gap modification changes model behavior, this account provides a principled basis for selecting gap interventions in similarity-based vision-language systems across evaluated downstream tasks.

</details>


### [9] [Hardware-Aware Functional Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks for Efficient Medical Image Enhancement and Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36134)
*Mohammad Sadegh Sirjani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FunKANLite，一种针对边缘医疗设备的硬件感知压缩模型，通过两阶段策略（FunKANLite-TR和FunKANLite-ST）显著减少参数量和计算量，同时保持高精度并降低能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始FunKAN模型在MRI伪影去除和解剖分割任务上精度领先，但其11.6M参数和8.7 GFLOPs的计算开销过大，无法部署于资源受限的边缘医疗设备。

Method: 采用两阶段硬件感知压缩方法：第一阶段FunKANLite-TR通过简化空间先验和替换ResBlock预测器减少2倍参数且不失精度；第二阶段FunKANLite-ST通过降低Hermite基秩、低秩分解空间先验和减半滤波器宽度，进一步将参数量减少5.6倍，GFLOPs减少3.7倍。

Result: FunKANLite-ST在BUSI、GlaS和CVC-ClinicDB数据集上的IoU损失不超过1.4个百分点，在IXI数据集上PSNR达到33.95 dB。在NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano和Raspberry Pi 5上，推理能耗降低高达68%，吞吐量提升2.9倍。

Conclusion: FunKANLite成功实现了FunKAN模型的高效压缩，使其适用于点护理（point-of-care）场景下的边缘医疗计算，兼顾了高精度与低功耗需求。

Abstract: Functional Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (FunKAN) achieve state-of-the-art accuracy on MRI Gibbs artifact removal and anatomical segmentation, but their 11.6 M parameters and 8.7 GFLOPs are too large for edge medical devices. We present FunKANLite, a two-stage, hardware-aware compression of FunKAN for point-of-care use. FunKANLite-TR reduces the spatial prior and replaces the ResBlock offset predictor with a depthwise-separable block. It has 1.9x fewer parameters than FunKAN and no loss in accuracy. We then distill FunKANLite-TR into FunKANLite-ST, which lowers the Hermite basis rank, factorizes the spatial prior into a low-rank form, and halves the filter widths. FunKANLite-ST has 5.6x fewer parameters and 3.7x fewer GFLOPs than FunKAN. It stays within 1.4 percentage points IoU of FunKAN on BUSI, GlaS, and CVC-ClinicDB, and reaches 33.95 dB PSNR on IXI. On an NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano and a Raspberry Pi 5, FunKANLite-ST reduces energy per inference by up to 68% and raises throughput by 2.9x.

</details>


### [10] [Xiaomi-OCR-0 Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36136)
*Xin Chen,Anan Du,Feng Feng,Pei Fu,Jian Luan,Longwei Xu,Shaojie Zhang,Hang Li,Heng Qu,Cheng Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Xiaomi-OCR-0 是一个统一的 0.8B 文档解析与 OCR 理解模型，通过自动化数据引擎构建的大规模语料库及渐进式训练策略（Q-Mask、持续预训练、混合强化学习），在多项基准测试中实现了卓越的 OCR 性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的紧凑型 OCR 专用视觉语言模型虽然文档解析能力强，但通常依赖高成本的标注数据，且主要侧重于视觉-文本重建，缺乏对文档内容的深层理解能力。

Method: 1. **数据构建**：利用自动数据引擎结合专家共识、渲染验证和目标合成，构建了约 1.7 亿样本的 OCR 中心语料库。
2. **模型基础**：基于 Qwen3.5-0.8B 进行初始化。
3. **训练策略**：采用渐进式训练食谱，包括基于 Q-Mask 的文本锚定、持续预训练以及混合任务强化学习（Mix-RL）。

Result: 在 Real5-OmniDocBench 上得分 95.24，OmniDocBench v1.6 上得分 96.83，Wild-OmniDocBench 上得分 87.94；在五个 OCR 导向的 VQA 基准测试中平均得分为 83.2。消融实验表明，充足的解析训练配合 OCR 中心理解监督可进一步提升文档解析效果。

Conclusion: Xiaomi-OCR-0 证明了通过大规模自动化数据和多阶段训练策略，可以在保持模型轻量级（0.8B）的同时，实现高效的文档解析与深度 OCR 语义理解的统一。

Abstract: Compact OCR-specific vision-language models achieve strong document parsing performance, but often rely on costly supervision and focus primarily on visual-text reconstruction. We introduce Xiaomi-OCR-0, a unified 0.8B model for document parsing and OCR-centric understanding. We build an approximately 170M-sample OCR-centric corpus using an automated data engine that combines expert consensus, render-based verification, and targeted synthesis. Starting from Qwen3.5-0.8B, our progressive training recipe combines Q-Mask-based text anchoring, continued pretraining, and mixed-task reinforcement learning (Mix-RL). Xiaomi-OCR-0 achieves 95.24 on Real5-OmniDocBench, 96.83 on OmniDocBench v1.6, and 87.94 on Wild-OmniDocBench, while reaching an average score of 83.2 across five OCR-oriented VQA benchmarks. Ablations further show that, with sufficient parsing training, OCR-centric understanding supervision provides additional gains for document parsing.
  Homepage: https://huggingface.co/spaces/SeerRay-Lab/Xiaomi-OCR-0.

</details>


### [11] [From Sharp Eyes to Expert Mind: Internalizing Expert Knowledge in MLLMs for Tampered Text Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36145)
*Kaiqing Lin,Songze Li,Shen Chen,Yunfei Guo,Xiaoye Qiu,Haodong Li,Taiping Yao,Bo Wang,Youchang Xiao,Bin Li,Shouhong Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出专家知识内化（EKI）框架，解决多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在篡改文本检测中缺乏细粒度感知的问题。通过两阶段训练将专家模型的视觉感知能力内化至MLLM，实现高精度与强泛化能力的统一，且在推理时无需外部专家模块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有专家模型虽能捕捉细微篡改痕迹但泛化性差，而多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）语义理解强但缺乏对细粒度取证伪影的敏感性。旨在将专家取证感知能力内化至MLLM，而非仅作为外部模块调用，以结合两者优势。

Method: 提出渐进式两阶段专家知识内化（EKI）框架：
1. 第一阶段：采用文本聚焦和图像聚焦策略，建立对小文本区域的精确空间关注，解决空间精度不匹配问题。
2. 第二阶段：提出取证通用表示对齐（FGRA）损失函数，使浅层LLM表示与预训练取证专家的表示对齐，在深层语义抽象稀释线索前获取细粒度伪影感知，解决感知粒度不匹配问题。

Result: 在多个域内和跨域基准测试上，EKI达到了最先进的性能，并展现出比现有基于专家模型和基于MLLM的方法更强的泛化能力。由于专家仅在训练阶段使用，推理效率与原始MLLM几乎一致，无需依赖外部专家。

Conclusion: 通过解决空间精度和感知粒度的双重不匹配问题，EKI成功将专家取证知识内化到MLLM中，实现了高效、高精度且具备强泛化能力的篡改文本检测方案。

Abstract: Tampered Text Detection (TTD) is essential for safeguarding document authenticity in security-critical workflows. Existing expert models are effective at capturing subtle manipulation traces but often generalize poorly across diverse document domains, while Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) offer stronger semantic understanding and transferability yet remain insensitive to fine-grained forensic artifacts. This complementarity motivates us to investigate how expert forensic perception can be internalized into an MLLM rather than merely accessed through an external module. We identify a fundamental Double Mismatch that hinders this goal: a Spatial Precision Mismatch between coarse visual tokens and tiny tampered regions, and a Perceptual Granularity Mismatch between semantics-oriented pre-training and low-level forensic perception. To address these challenges, we propose Expert Knowledge Internalization (EKI), a progressive two-stage framework that transfers forensic expertise into the MLLM itself. In Stage 1, Text-Focused and Image-Focused strategies establish precise spatial focus on small text regions. In Stage 2, the proposed Forensic-General Representation Alignment (FGRA) loss aligns shallow LLM representations with those of a pre-trained forensic expert, enabling the model to acquire fine-grained artifact perception before such cues are diluted by deeper semantic abstraction. Extensive experiments on multiple in-domain and cross-domain benchmarks demonstrate that EKI achieves state-of-the-art performance and stronger generalization than existing expert-model-based and MLLM-based methods. Moreover, the expert is required only during training, allowing the resulting MLLM to maintain inference efficiency nearly identical to the vanilla model without relying on any external expert at inference.

</details>


### [12] [Boosting Metric Depth Completion via Training-Free Adaptive Response Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36168)
*Mia Zhang,Jizong Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种自适应响应几何框架，通过统一深度、对数深度和视差坐标并估计显式深度相关增益，解决了传统深度校准中刚性仿射假设导致的系统标定误差问题。该方法在训练-free条件下实现了优于现有方法的精度指标（宏观AbsRel 0.0301，宏观NMed 14.04°）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有利用视觉基础模型作为几何先验进行深度补全的方法，通常依赖预定义坐标系中的刚性仿射假设来对齐先验与真实度量尺度，这导致了系统性的标定误差。此外，深度校准的线性度依赖于响应坐标的选择，固定选择（如深度、对数深度或视差）限制了模型的适应性。

Method: 引入“自适应响应几何”概念，将深度、对数深度或视差的固定选择转化为图像级未知变量。构建了一个连续的响应族以统一这些坐标，并定义了显式的深度相关增益。推导了响应-梯度关系，并在度量空间中估计响应参数。最后，通过Hard-Dirichlet残差重建完成校准后的先验修复。

Result: 在故意不完整的度量观测下，该免训练（training-free）管道实现了宏观绝对相对误差（AbsRel）为0.0301，宏观非中位数角度误差（NMed）为14.04°。相比PriorDA、LDCM和Any2Full等基线方法，该方案在综合评价指标上均有所提升。

Conclusion: 提出的自适应响应几何方法有效解决了深度校准中的系统性误差问题，并通过线性诊断分析了不同响应选择对深度关系及度量误差的影响，证明了其在深度补全任务中的优越性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Depth completion aims to recover dense metric depth from sparse sensor measurements, increasingly leveraging visual foundation models as geometric priors. However, aligning these priors to true metric scale typically relies on rigid affine assumptions in predefined coordinate systems, leaving systematic calibration errors. Linearity in depth calibration depends on the response coordinate. We introduce adaptive response geometry, which makes the fixed choice of depth, log depth, or disparity an image-level unknown. A continuous response family unifies these coordinates and defines an explicit depth-dependent gain. We derive the response-gradient relation and estimate the response parameters in metric space. Hard-Dirichlet residual reconstruction completes the calibrated prior. Under deliberately incomplete metric observations, the training-free pipeline achieves macro AbsRel 0.0301 and macro NMed 14.04°, improving both aggregate measures over PriorDA, LDCM, and Any2Full. Linearity diagnostics examine how the selected response changes the depth relation and its metric error.

</details>


### [13] [Exploring Learning Models for Topological Relationship Recognition from Image Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36172)
*Saptak Das,Monidipa Das*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对图像拓扑关系识别缺乏数据集和评估标准的痛点，构建了包含11,000+标注图像的开源数据集。通过对比传统机器学习与深度学习模型（如VGG16、InceptionResNetV2），发现基于迁移学习的深度学习方法在验证集上达到89.55%的准确率，显著优于传统方法，证明了其在空间推理中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决地理信息系统（GIS）、生物医学成像和机器人领域中物体间拓扑关系（如接触、重叠、分离、包含）识别的问题。现有研究因缺乏高质量数据集和明确的评估标准而进展缓慢。

Method: 1. 构建新数据集：收集并标注超过11,000张图像，涵盖主要拓扑关系。
2. 特征提取：使用分割、轮廓检测和灰度归一化技术提取稳健的特征向量。
3. 模型训练与评估：测试传统机器学习模型（Naive Bayes, KNN, Random Forest, SVM）和深度学习模型（VGG16, InceptionResNetV2）。

Result: 深度学习模型表现优异，其中VGG16在验证集上的准确率达到89.55%，显著高于传统机器学习模型。

Conclusion: 迁移学习在图像拓扑关系分析中具有强大潜力。本研究提供的数据集为未来的空间推理和拓扑分类研究建立了新的基准。

Abstract: Figuring out how objects relate to each other, like whether they touch, overlap, stay completely separate or one sits inside another, matters a lot in fields like GIS, biomedical imaging, and robotics. Even though machine learning has come a long way, people haven't really focused on spotting these topological relationships in images. The main roadblocks? Not enough good datasets and no clear way to measure results. So, we rolled up our sleeves and built a new dataset. It's pretty sizable: over 11,000 labelled images showing all those essential relationships. We ran tests with some classic machine learning models, Naive Bayes, KNN, Random Forest, SVM, and Artificial Neural Networks, and threw in some deep learning stars like VGG16 and InceptionResNetV2. For the dataset itself, we used segmentation, contour detection, and grayscale normalization to tease out solid feature vectors. The results? Deep learning methods, especially VGG16, pulled ahead, with validation accuracy hitting 89.55%. That's a big jump compared to the traditional models. This shows how powerful transfer learning is for analyzing topological relationships in images, and it gives researchers a new standard to aim for in future work on spatial reasoning and topological classification.

</details>


### [14] [FD-AA: A Lightweight Focal-Diffuse And Attenuation-Aware Head for Incidental Abdominal Abnormality Detection in Chest CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36189)
*Haoyan Ding,Kritika Iyer,Halid Yerebakan,Zhenyu Bu,Chushu Shen,Peiyu Duan,Xinyuan Zheng,Sepehr Farhand,Xueqi Guo,Chaowei Wu,Yoshihisa Shinagawa,Gerardo Hermosillo Valadez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为FD-AA的轻量级器官感知分类头，用于增强冻结的3D CT编码器在常规胸部CT中检测腹部偶然异常的能力。该方法通过保留稀疏焦点证据和捕捉弥散异常模式，在多个数据集上实现了SOTA性能，并验证了其在不同特征表示下的泛化有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规胸部CT扫描常包含上腹部结构，其中可能存在具有临床意义的偶然性异常。由于不同器官的空间范围和衰减模式各异，从这些器官中提取特征以检测此类发现存在挑战。

Method: 提出了FD-AA（一种轻量级器官感知分类头），可适配于冻结的3D CT编码器。其核心机制包括：1）每个器官内的衰减感知模块，用于保留稀疏的焦点证据；2）掩码广义均值池化，用于捕捉弥散的异常模式。

Result: 在7个腹部器官中，FD-AA结合Pillar-0编码器在CT-RATE测试集（AUC=0.798）和外部RAD-ChestCT数据集（AUC=0.713）上均达到最先进水平（SOTA）。相比直接使用冻结的Pillar-0进行分类，宏AUC/AP从0.763/0.346提升至0.798/0.405（p=0.034/0.016）。此外，该方法在MedicalNet、CT-CLIP和ResNet等其他冻结编码器上也表现出显著的AUC提升（分别为+9.8%、+14.7%和+3.7%）。

Conclusion: 将焦点-弥散聚合与明确的HU（亨氏单位）证据相结合，能有效提升腹部偶然异常检测的性能，且该策略在不同特征表示中具有广泛的适用性和有效性。

Abstract: Routine chest CT captures upper-abdominal structures that may contain clinically relevant incidental abnormalities. Detecting these findings requires feature extraction from organs with different spatial extents and attenuation patterns. We propose FD-AA, a lightweight organ-aware classification head adaptable for frozen 3-D CT encoders. Within each organ, an attenuation-aware module preserves sparse focal evidence, while masked generalized-mean pooling captures diffuse anomaly patterns. In seven abdominal organs, FD-AA with Pillar-0 achieved state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance in both the CT-RATE test set (AUC = 0.798) and the external RAD-ChestCT dataset (AUC = 0.713). More specifically, FD-AA improved macro AUC/AP from 0.763/0.346 to 0.798/0.405 over direct classification using frozen Pillar-0 only (p = 0.034/0.016). Such performance gain generalizes across multiple frozen encoders (AUC improvement on MedicalNet +9.8%, CT-CLIP +14.7%, ResNet +3.7%), demonstrating the effectiveness of FD-AA across different feature representations. These results support the effectiveness of integrating focal-diffuse aggregation with explicit HU evidence for incidental abdominal abnormality detection.

</details>


### [15] [PreviewDiff: Multimodal Critic-Guided Search over Diffusion Latents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36199)
*Vighnesh Subramaniam,Boris Katz,Brian Cheung,Chun-Liang Li,Tomas Pfister,Yale Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PreviewDiff，一种无需训练的测试时搜索方法，利用多模态评审器在扩散模型去噪过程中提供中间反馈，通过分支和重新去噪优化生成结果，显著优于传统的Best-of-N采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在忠实于提示词（如物体数量、属性绑定、空间关系）方面存在不足；现有的Best-of-N采样仅在最终输出中选择，无法在生成过程中修复有潜力的轨迹。

Method: PreviewDiff在选定的去噪检查点解码部分预览，利用多模态裁判进行评分和批评，根据自然语言反馈对语义提示编辑和局部重去噪的潜在变量分支进行评分，并选择性推进。

Result: 在图像和视频生成基准测试中，PreviewDiff一致优于预算匹配的Best-of-N选择和强标量搜索基线；早期干预和增加搜索宽度带来最大增益。

Conclusion: 多模态反馈不仅可作为最终验证器，还可作为去噪过程中的主动控制器，显著提升生成的保真度。

Abstract: Diffusion models can produce striking images and videos, but they still struggle with the compositional details that make a generation faithful to a prompt, such as object counts, attribute binding, spatial relations, and temporally grounded actions. A common way to improve prompt satisfaction is to spend more compute at test time through Best-of-N sampling, but final-sample selection is fixed. Best-of-N can only choose among completed outputs and cannot repair a promising trajectory before it fails. We introduce PreviewDiff, a training-free test-time search method that turns diffusion sampling from scalar search into a multimodal critic-guided search over intermediate latents. At selected denoising checkpoints, PreviewDiff decodes a partial preview, asks a multimodal judge to score and critique it, and uses the resulting natural-language feedback to branch over semantic prompt edits and locally re-noised latent continuations. These branches are then scored and selectively rolled forward, allowing verifier compute to guide generation while the sample is still editable. Across image and video generation benchmarks, PreviewDiff consistently improves over budget-matched Best-of-N selection and strong scalar-search baselines. Ablations show that earlier interventions and increased search width provide the largest gains, while deeper search and additional semantic variants offer complementary improvements. PreviewDiff demonstrates that multimodal feedback is most useful not only as a final verifier, but as an active controller inside the denoising process.

</details>


### [16] [Sparse-View Interpretable 3D Animal Behavior Representations for Neural Encoding and Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36217)
*Xinming Dai,Qihang Jin,Tianshu Tan,Baiyuan Chen,Hanrui Lyu,Lenny Aharon,Kyle Daruwalla,Xun Helen Hou,Matthew R. Whiteway,Liam Paninski,Yizi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SABLE框架，利用几何归纳偏置和自监督学习，从极稀疏视角的视频中重建3D动物行为表征，无需3D标签即可实现高精度三维恢复，并具有跨物种零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有行为表征方法存在局限：姿态估计丢弃关键点位之外的丰富信息，而非线性视频嵌入缺乏可解释性。需要一种能精确、结构化地表征行为且保持可解释性的新方法。

Method: 提出SABLE（Sparse-view Animal Behavior Latent Embeddings），这是一个自监督框架。通过增强多视图Transformer，引入单目深度和姿态估计的先验知识作为几何归纳偏置，从而从极稀疏视角重构3D动物行为并学习显式的3D潜在结构。

Result: 1. 无需地面真值3D标签，仅凭两视图视频即可可靠恢复3D行为，优于现有最先进（SOTA）方法；2. 在国际脑实验室（IBL）和Cheese3D数据集上，其学习的3D表征在神经编码和解码任务中达到或超越SOTA性能；3. 预训练后的模型具有零样本泛化能力，无需针对新动物进行校准或重训练即可直接应用。

Conclusion: SABLE建立了感知3D的视频嵌入，能够捕捉复杂的行为模式，为研究大脑与行为之间的关系开辟了新的途径。

Abstract: A deeper understanding of brain function requires a precise, structured characterization of behavior.Yet, extracting behavioral representations from video in a form suitable for scientific analysis remains a fundamental challenge. Many prior studies represent behavior via pose estimation or nonlinear video embeddings. However, pose tracking discards rich information beyond predefined keypoints, while nonlinear video embeddings lack interpretability. We address this limitation with SABLE (Sparse-view Animal Behavior Latent Embeddings), a self-supervised framework that leverages a geometric inductive bias to learn behavior representations.By augmenting a multi-view transformer with priors from monocular depth and pose estimation, SABLE reconstructs 3D animal behavior from extremely sparse views while learning explicit 3D latent structure. Without ground-truth 3D labels, it reliably recovers 3D behavior from two-view videos, whereas state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods fail or yield degenerate solutions. Across the International Brain Lab and Cheese3D datasets, we demonstrate that SABLE learns 3D representations that match or exceed prior SOTA performance in neural encoding and decoding. Once pretrained across animals, SABLE serves as an off-the-shelf model that generalizes zero-shot to unseen animals without animal-specific calibration or retraining. Our method establishes 3D-aware video embeddings that capture complex behavior, opening new avenues for studying brain-behavior relationships.

</details>


### [17] [LeRF: Learning Reference Coordinate Frames for Perspective Taking Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36219)
*Bang Xiao,Wenqi Jia,Ozgur Kara,Tiancheng Shen,Yibo Yang,Bolin Lai,Junho Kim,James Matthew Rehg*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LeRF框架，通过训练视觉语言模型(VLMs)构建和使用显式参考坐标系来解决视角转换难题。LeRF能判断是否需要坐标系，预测其原点和朝向，并通过轻量级渲染器将坐标系叠加到图像上辅助推理，无需外部3D重建。结合监督微调与强化学习，在多个基准测试中显著提升了VLM的视角依赖推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管视觉语言模型(VLMs)的空间推理能力日益增强，但在“视角转换”(perspective taking)任务中仍表现不佳。当查询要求从非相机视角（如其他实体或想象观察者）进行推理时，模型往往默认回到相机视角，导致推理错误。

Method: 1. 提出LeRF框架：让VLMs构建并使用显式参考坐标系进行视角依赖推理。
2. 决策机制：给定图像和查询，模型判断是否需要引入坐标系。
3. 坐标生成：若需要，定位参考实体并预测坐标系原点及以实体为中心的参考帧。
4. 可视化辅助：使用轻量级渲染器将坐标系叠加到图像上，作为后续推理的视觉线索，避免外部感知模型或显式3D重建。
5. 训练策略：先通过监督微调教授选择性工具调用和坐标系预测，再通过空间VQA对的强化学习优化基于坐标系的推理过程。

Result: 1. 在多种视角转换基准测试中，LeRF consistently 优于其骨干模型，并在开源方法中表现强劲。
2. 进一步评估显示，LeRF在参考系定位和方向估计方面也有显著提升，证明了所学参考系在视角依赖推理中的有效性。

Conclusion: LeRF通过引入显式参考坐标系和可视化反馈机制，有效解决了VLMs在视角转换任务中的局限性，提升了模型在非相机视角下的空间推理能力和准确性。

Abstract: Perspective taking is a fundamental component of spatial intelligence, requiring models interpret spatial relations from a specified viewpoint, such as that of another entity or an imagined observer. Despite the increasing spatial reasoning capabilities of Vision-Language Models (VLMs), they still struggle with perspective taking, often defaulting to the camera viewpoint when a query requires reasoning from a different perspective. We introduce Learning Reference Coordinate Frames for Perspective Taking (LeRF), a framework that trains VLMs to construct and use explicit reference frames for viewpoint-dependent reasoning. Given an image and a query, LeRF decides whether a coordinate frame is necessary. If so, it grounds the reference entity and predicts the frame's origin and entity-centered reference frame. A lightweight renderer overlays the frame onto the image, enabling subsequent reasoning over these visual cues without external perception models or explicit 3D reconstruction. To learn this process, we first perform supervised fine-tuning to teach selective tool invocation and reference coordinate frame prediction, followed by reinforcement learning on spatial VQA pairs to improve frame-guided reasoning. Across diverse perspective-taking benchmarks, LeRF consistently improves over its backbone and achieves strong performance against existing open-source methods. Further evaluations also show improved reference-frame grounding and orientation estimation, supporting the effectiveness of learned reference frames for viewpoint-dependent reasoning.

</details>


### [18] [Mutually Adversarial Self-Training with Evolving Data for Unified Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36224)
*Wentao Zhou,Weijie Gan,Jiayun Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MATE框架，通过强化学习让多模态模型的生成与理解分支相互对抗并自我训练，提升双向性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一多模态模型（UMMs）常利用生成与理解的互逆关系进行合作式自训练，但本研究旨在探索更具挑战性的对抗式自我训练机制，以进一步提升模型能力。

Method: 提出MATE（Mutually Adversarial self-Training with Evolving data），一种基于强化学习的后训练框架。该框架让生成和理解分支轮流担任“挑战者”和“求解者”，通过一致性筛选候选描述/图像，并针对表现最差的候选进行训练。挑战数据随模型训练动态演化，实现数据空间中的自我博弈。

Result: 在Janus-Pro-1B模型上，MATE使GenEval提升2.4分，DPG-Bench提升1.7分，9个理解基准平均提升0.7分，同时增强了图文循环的一致性。

Conclusion: MATE通过对抗式自我训练和动态演化的挑战数据，有效提升了统一多模模型在生成和理解任务上的性能及一致性，无需额外训练独立对抗器。

Abstract: Unified multimodal models (UMMs) combine image generation and visual understanding in a shared backbone. Since generation and understanding are inverse tasks, recent studies self-train UMMs by letting the two branches cooperatively supervise each other. We introduce MATE (Mutually Adversarial self-Training with Evolving data), a reinforcement-learning-based post-training framework in which the two branches instead challenge each other, and the challenges evolve as the model trains. MATE lets generation and understanding take turns to be challenger and solver. Given an image, the understanding branch proposes several candidate descriptions that the generation branch must turn back into similar images, and vice versa. The candidates are screened for consistency with the image or prompt they were proposed from, and the solver is trained on the candidate it handles worst. The adversary thus comes from the model's own outputs, and no separate adversary is trained. Moreover, the candidates that defeat one branch become the sources of the next challenges to the other in the next epoch, which keeps the challenges evolving with the model and turns the training into self-play in data space. On Janus-Pro-1B, MATE improves GenEval by 2.4 points, DPG-Bench by 1.7 points, and the average over nine understanding benchmarks by 0.7 points, while strengthening consistency across repeated image-text cycles.

</details>


### [19] [Think Before You Restore: Risk-Aware Manchu Manuscript Restoration with Stroke-Guided Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36243)
*Mingqiu Liang,Dongdong Wang,Siyang Lu,Ting Huang,Yingjun Qi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SAGE-Restore框架，通过评估修复需求并引导像素级选择，解决满文古籍全页盲复原中的过修复问题，在恢复退化区域的同时高保真保留完整内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史满文手稿的全页盲复原面临标注稀缺、退化区域未知及笔画粘连脆弱等挑战。通用模型虽能提升视觉质量，但常修改完好内容导致“过修复”，缺乏对内容保全的兼顾。

Method: 提出SAGE-Restore（Stroke-Aware Gated rEstoration）选择性复原框架：1. 编码器结合外观与笔画结构线索预测补丁级修复概率；2. 利用该概率条件化生成修复候选项；3. 将修复逻辑值细化为像素级软门控，选择性控制候选项的应用位置。

Result: 引入感知保真的评估协议，联合衡量退化区域恢复、完好内容保留及其平衡性。SAGE-Restore取得最高R-Recovery (0.463)和RFS (0.626)，同时保持高U-Fidelity (0.968)。

Conclusion: SAGE-Restore有效平衡了图像复原与内容保护，证明了选择性门控机制在复杂历史文档复原任务中的优越性。

Abstract: Full-page blind restoration of historical Manchu manuscripts is challenging due to scarce annotations, unknown degradation regions, and fragile connected strokes. Generic restoration models may improve visual quality but often modify intact content, leading to over-restoration. We propose SAGE-Restore (Stroke-Aware Gated rEstoration), a selective restoration framework that first assesses where restoration is needed and then uses this assessment to guide restoration candidate generation and pixel-level selection. Its encoder predicts patch-level repair probabilities from complementary appearance and stroke-structural cues to condition restoration candidate generation, while the corresponding repair logits are refined into a pixel-level soft gate that selectively controls where the restoration candidate is applied. We further introduce a fidelity-aware evaluation protocol that jointly measures degraded-region recovery, intact-content preservation, and their balance. SAGE-Restore achieves the highest R-Recovery (0.463) and RFS (0.626), while maintaining high U-Fidelity (0.968), demonstrating an effective balance between restoration and content preservation.

</details>


### [20] [Representation by Design in Generation: Cross-View Class-Token Alignment in Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36348)
*Xiaoyu Wu,Yifei Wang,Chen Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种结合交叉视图类令牌对齐的扩散模型预训练框架，在保持生成质量的同时显著提升语义表示能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决生成学习与表示学习之间的不对称性问题：传统方法中语义表示仅作为生成的副产品或工具，未能被直接优化以增强其表达能力。

Method: 在流匹配（flow matching）基础上引入自监督机制，借鉴DINO和iBOT思想，构建双时间步、独立加噪的双视图观测；通过停止梯度的EMA教师目标对两个视图的学生类令牌表示进行对齐，并与原有的流匹配及局部块目标联合优化。

Result: 1. 表示能力提升：ImageNet线性探测准确率提升9.4%（类令牌）和10.1%（平均池化块令牌），VOC2012分割mIoU提升3.6；2. 生成质量维持/提升：保持可比ImageNet FID，文本到图像生成FID从2.52降至2.37。

Conclusion: 语义表示不应仅是生成的副产品，而可作为扩散预训练中与生成同等重要的第一类能力进行直接优化。

Abstract: Generative and representation learning remain asymmetrically connected: semantic representations are used to improve diffusion generation, whereas the models' own representations are often treated as a by-product of synthesis. We ask whether diffusion models can instead be trained to learn substantially stronger semantic representations without sacrificing generation quality. SelfFlow takes a step in this direction by introducing self-supervised patch alignment into flow matching, but its main gains remain in faster convergence and improved generation. Inspired by DINO and iBOT, we extend this framework with cross-view class-token alignment to further strengthen semantic representations. Specifically, we form two independently noised, dual-timestep observations of each image and align each student class-token representation with the stop-gradient EMA-teacher target from the other observation. This objective is optimized jointly with the inherited flow-matching and local patch objectives. Notably, although the additional objective acts only on the class token, it strengthens both class-token and patch representations. Compared with a matched two-view baseline, ImageNet linear-probing accuracy improves by 9.4\% using the class token and 10.1\% using mean-pooled patch tokens, while frozen-backbone VOC2012 segmentation improves by 3.6 mIoU. These representation gains are achieved while maintaining comparable ImageNet generation FID. In text-to-image training, the same objective also improves generation FID, reducing it from 2.52 to 2.37 at matched checkpoints. Our results show that representation need not remain a by-product of generation or merely a tool for improving it: it can be directly optimized as a first-class capability of diffusion pretraining alongside generation.

</details>


### [21] [StructRL: Online Structured Reinforcement Learning for Long-Horizon Vision-Language-Action Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36352)
*Ziyi Yin,Sangmin Woo,Kang Zhou,Sungyeon Kim,Aosong Feng,Haibo Ding,Jun Huan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出StructRL框架，通过构建可验证的子任务结构化和中间奖励，解决VLA模型在长视距任务中因终端监督稀疏而导致的性能瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线强化学习方法仅提供任务完成后的终端奖励，这种稀疏监督无法区分早期失败与取得实质性进展的轨迹，导致VLA模型在处理需要多次依赖操作的长视距任务时表现不佳。

Method: 提出StructRL在线强化学习框架：将任务分解为可验证的子任务；仅在先决子任务完成后授予中间奖励；并根据完成进度对奖励进行缩放。

Result: 在RoboCasa365和LIBERO-Long基准测试上，使用GR00T-N1.5和pi 0.5模型进行评估，StructRL consistently优于所有评估的在线RL基线方法。

Conclusion: 可验证的结构化中间奖励能有效提升长视距视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的后期训练效果。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models perform well on shorter-horizon manipulation tasks but still struggle with long-horizon tasks that require multiple dependent manipulations from a single command. Online reinforcement learning (RL) can improve these policies through environment interaction, yet many existing methods provide reward only after the complete task succeeds. However, such terminal supervision is sparse and does not distinguish early failures from rollouts that make substantial partial progress. We propose StructRL, an online RL framework that constructs structured intermediate supervision from verifiable subtask completions. StructRL decomposes each task into verifiable subtasks, grants intermediate rewards only after the prerequisite subtasks have been completed, and scales each reward according to completion pace. Across RoboCasa365 and LIBERO-Long with GR00T-N1.5 and pi 0.5, StructRL consistently outperforms evaluated online RL baselines. These results show that verifiable, structured intermediate rewards improve long-horizon VLA post-training. Code is available at https://github.com/amazon-science/StructRL.

</details>


### [22] [Compress to Remember: Learning Compact Memory via On-Policy Distillation for Long Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36364)
*Xiaoyu Wu,Weihang Guo,Yifei Wang,Xinze Feng,Lydia E. Kavraki,Zhiwei Steven Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PACC方法，通过冻结的视频生成器作为教师和学生进行策略蒸馏，训练压缩器将历史帧信息聚合为紧凑的记忆token，从而解决长视频生成中的上下文退化问题，无需修改底层生成器即可提升长期记忆能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准视频生成器无法原生地将历史上下文压缩为可复用的记忆token，随着生成过程推进，不断增长的上下文导致早期帧信息难以保留（长上下文退化）。基于关键帧的方法虽能保留部分过去帧，但可能丢弃未来生成所需的信息。

Method: 提出预测对齐上下文压缩（Prediction-Aligned Context Compaction, PACC）。使用学习到的压缩器将过去多帧信息聚合为紧凑的记忆token。采用在线策略蒸馏进行训练：利用同一个冻结的视频生成器，当以压缩记忆为条件时作为“学生”，当以完整历史为条件时作为“教师”。学生在每个去噪步骤对相同噪声输入生成延续，教师提供目标；仅更新压缩器以使学生预测与教师目标对齐。

Result: 在MBench基准上，PACC在Causal-rCM指标上比最强基线高出6.63分，在Causal Forcing上高出3.19分。在VBench-Long上使用MovieGen提示词评估显示，PACC能生成品质量与基线竞争性的长达一分钟的视频。

Conclusion: 通过学习将历史上下文压缩为紧凑表示，可以在不修改底层生成器的情况下改善长视频生成的记忆能力。

Abstract: Standard video generators do not natively compact historical context into reusable memory tokens. As generation continues, the growing history makes it increasingly difficult to retain information from earlier frames due to long-context degradation. Key-frame-based approaches address this challenge by retaining selected past frames, but can discard information needed for future generation. Rather than relying on frame selection alone, we study whether a frozen video generator can supply the supervision needed to learn a compact representation of the history. We propose Prediction-Aligned Context Compaction (PACC), which uses a learned compressor to aggregate information across past frames into compact memory tokens. We train the compressor through on-policy distillation, using the same frozen generator both as a student when conditioned on compressed memory and as a teacher when conditioned on the full history. The student generates continuations, while the teacher provides targets for the same noisy inputs at each denoising step. Only the compressor is updated to align the student's predictions with these targets. We evaluate PACC on MBench, which jointly measures memory-event coverage and consistency. PACC outperforms the strongest baseline by 6.63 points on Causal-rCM and 3.19 points on Causal Forcing. Evaluation on VBench-Long using MovieGen prompts further shows that PACC produces minute-long videos with generation quality competitive with baselines. Together, these results show that learning to compact historical context can improve long-video memory without modifying the underlying generator.

</details>


### [23] [OTT3R: Multi-View 3D Reconstruction and Fast Dataset Generation at 1% Compute](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36374)
*Brandon Leblanc,Charalambos Poullis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出OTT3R，一种通过知识蒸馏将大型3D重建模型压缩至轻量级学生的框架，解决了高成本和依赖缓慢COLMAP的问题。在双GPU工作站上训练，实现了9.4倍压缩和7倍加速推理，并提供了比COLMAP更快、更可靠的全自动伪标签生成管道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的前馈3D重建模型虽性能优异但成本高昂，难以普及及部署到边缘设备；同时，缺乏类似COLMAP的高效神经3D伪标签生成系统，导致生成3D监督数据速度慢且不可靠。

Method: 1. **知识蒸馏**：将拥有959M参数的教师模型（$\pi^3$）蒸馏为102M参数的学生模型，仅在2块消费级GPU的单个工作站上完成训练，计算量仅为VGGT的1.6%。
2. **集成伪标签流水线**：开发了一种高吞吐量替代方案，能在3.5小时内为66.7万张图像生成密集逐像素点图和SE(3)相机位姿，克服了COLMAP在某些序列上失败的问题。
3. **领域专业化微调**：对通用学生模型进行极低成本（0.2%计算量）的专业化训练，以优化特定任务表现。

Result: 1. **效率提升**：模型压缩9.4倍，推理速度提升高达7倍。
2. **伪标签优势**：生成的伪标签覆盖了所有测试序列（包括COLMAP失败的案例），且在7-Scenes数据集上，专业化学生模型比COLMAP准确率高4倍，吞吐量高出980倍，完成度接近教师模型水平。
3. **局限性**：通用学生模型在分布外多视图几何任务上未能完全达到教师模型的性能水平。

Conclusion: OTT3R成功证明了通过知识蒸馏可以显著降低3D重建模型的部署门槛和计算成本，同时提出的伪标签流水线提供了一种高效、可靠的替代传统SfM工具的方案，推动了3D重建技术在资源受限环境下的应用。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D reconstruction models have achieved impressive performance by scaling model and dataset size, but their cost excludes most research groups and precludes edge deployment. Additionally, generating 3D supervision without sensors still relies on slow, unreliable Structure-from-Motion, as the community lacks a COLMAP-like system for neural 3D pseudo-label generation. We present OTT3R (RGB-Only Tiny Transformer for 3D Reconstruction), a knowledge distillation framework that addresses both problems on a single workstation equipped with 2 GPUs. Distilling $π^3$ (959M parameters) into a 102M-parameter student yields 9.4$\times$ compression and up to 7$\times$ faster inference, trained at 1.6% of VGGT's training compute. An integrated pseudo-label pipeline offers a reliable, high-throughput alternative to COLMAP, generating dense per-pixel point maps and SE(3) camera poses for a 667K-image corpus in 3.5 hours on two commodity GPUs and succeeding on every sequence we tested, including those where COLMAP fails. The general student tracks the teacher on in-distribution monocular depth and, zero-shot, outperforms COLMAP on 7-Scenes and on DTU completion, but it does not replace the teacher on out-of-distribution multi-view geometry. The deployable artifact is the domain-specialized student: after specialization at 0.2% compute, it is 4$\times$ more accurate than COLMAP on 7-Scenes at 980$\times$ throughput, with near-teacher completion. Code is available at https://github.com/TheFourthKaramazov/OTT3R

</details>


### [24] [LEGO-Anything: Coding Agents for 3D Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36380)
*Xirui Li,Peng Shi,Mingwen Dong,Sheng Zhang,Zhuoyan Xu,Dongkyu Lee,Shuaichen Chang,Yi Xiang,Lin Pan,Jiarong Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LEGO-Anything框架，通过图像生成可编辑的Blender代码场景；发布LEGO-Bench基准测试；发现当前AI代理在场景重建中存在初始化弱、迭代倒退和自我评估不可靠等问题；引入LEGO-Plugin提升性能；证明程序化场景可用于视觉任务但精度尚不及专用模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的单图3D重建通常输出固定的渲染结果或网格，缺乏可解释性和可编辑性。研究旨在探索将3D场景表示为“显式场景程序”（如代码），以便用户能够检查、编辑和查询场景内容，从而提升3D内容的实用价值。

Method: 1. 构建LEGO-Anything框架：一个图像到代码（Image-to-Code）系统，利用编码智能体迭代编写、执行Blender代码，并基于检查结果修正程序。
2. 建立LEGO-Bench基准：包含104个室内外场景的208张图像，基于模拟器进行自动评估，指标包括伪影有效性、可见表面几何形状和渲染外观。
3. 开发LEGO-Plugin：一种无需训练的插件，用于控制迭代场景构建过程，解决智能体重复操作中的问题。
4. 验证应用潜力：在LEGO-World中测试从GPT-6-astra重建的场景中提取物体检测、实例掩码和相对深度的能力。

Result: 1. GPT-6-astra在所有评估的智能体中表现最佳，室内得分53.4%，室外得分39.6%。
2. 分析显示主要瓶颈在于：场景初始化能力弱、迭代过程中的编辑出现退化、自我评估不可靠。
3. LEGO-Plugin使所有六种评估模型的总体得分相对提升了最高62.7%。
4. 虽然程序化场景能支持基础视觉任务（非平凡性能），但其精度远低于专用的视觉模型。

Conclusion: 通过代码表示3D场景具有巨大潜力，允许对自然图像进行结构化查询和操作。然而，当前的编码智能体在几何和外观的忠实恢复上仍存在显著差距。LEGO-Plugin等改进措施有效提升了性能，但要达到与专用视觉模型相当的水平，仍需进一步的技术突破。

Abstract: A 3D scene reconstructed from a single image is most useful when represented not as a rendering or a fixed 3D output, but as an explicit scene program whose execution yields a scene that can be inspected, edited, and queried. We present LEGO-Anything, an Image-to-Code framework in which a coding agent iteratively writes and executes Blender code, inspects scenes and renderings, and revises the program. To evaluate end-to-end scene recovery, we introduce LEGO-Bench, a simulator-grounded benchmark with 208 images from 104 diverse indoor and outdoor scenes. LEGO-Bench separately scores artifact validity, visible-surface geometry, and rendered appearance. Its simulator-grounded design enables extensibility and precise automatic evaluation. Among evaluated agents, GPT-6-astra achieves the strongest overall results, with 53.4% indoor and 39.6% outdoor scores, yet substantial gaps remain between delivering valid scene artifacts and faithfully recovering scene geometry and appearance. Analysis of agent construction trajectories reveals three recurring issues: weak scene initialization, regressive edits during iteration, and unreliable self-evaluation. These findings motivate LEGO-Plugin, a training-free harness plugin for more controlled iterative scene construction, which improves all six evaluated models, with relative gains of up to 62.7% in overall score. Finally, we test whether reconstructed scenes can represent natural images and support vision tasks. In LEGO-World, we derive object detections, instance masks, and relative depth as deterministic queries on scenes reconstructed by GPT-6-astra. These readouts show non-trivial performance across all three tasks but fall well short of specialized vision models, suggesting that program-constructed scenes from current coding agents are a promising but not yet sufficiently precise representation of natural images.

</details>


### [25] [Beyond the Timeline: Augmenting Long-Video Memory with Grounded Entity Biographies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38155)
*Hui Ren,Lei Fan,Henry Pao,Han Guo,Zeeshan Zia,Ying Chen,Alexander Schwing,Gang Hua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Grounded Entity Biographies (GEB)框架，通过视觉锚定的身份关联将长视频中同一实体的观察整合为可检索的“传记”，解决跨时段实体身份识别难题，在EgoLifeQA等基准测试中显著提升问答准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以处理长视频中涉及同一物体的跨小时或天级事件连接；文本描述和实体可能无法唯一确定物理身份（不同物体共享描述，或同一物体观测分散），导致检索相关事件无法还原特定实体的完整“传记”。

Method: 构建Grounded Entity Biographies (GEB)长视频记忆框架：1) 在记忆构建阶段，通过视觉特征将同一物理实例的观察分组并建立身份链接，形成保留上下文的可检索“传记”；2) 在问答阶段，结合传记与情景证据进行检索，使模型能沿身份链接追踪实体。

Result: 在四个基准（含全天及周级录像）上优于先前记忆框架；在EgoLifeQA达到72.0%准确率（比最佳结果高4.4个百分点）；消融实验证实视觉身份关联和传记阅读均贡献增益，仅靠额外描述无法完全恢复性能。

Conclusion: GEB通过视觉锚定的实体身份关联有效解决了长视频问答中的实体追踪问题，提升了多选择与开放式问答的性能，证明了 grounded identity association 和 biography reading 的关键作用。

Abstract: Answering questions about long videos often requires connecting events involving the same objects across hours or days. Chronological descriptions and text-derived entities can leave physical identity unresolved: different objects may share a description, while observations of the same object remain disconnected across events. Retrieving relevant events therefore does not necessarily recover the "biography" of the particular entity a question concerns. To address this, we introduce Grounded Entity Biographies (GEB), a long-video memory framework that groups visually grounded observations of the same physical instance across clips into retrievable biographies while preserving the context of each moment. During question answering, the biography is retrieved alongside episodic evidence, allowing the model to follow an entity through events using identity links established during memory construction. Evaluations across four benchmarks, including day-long and week-long recordings, demonstrate improvements over prior memory frameworks in both multiple-choice and open-ended question answering. On EgoLifeQA, GEB achieves 72.0% accuracy, 4.4 percentage points above the best published result. Ablations show that grounded identity association and biography reading both contribute to the gains, which additional descriptions alone do not fully recover.

</details>


### [26] [Stealth Is a Relation, Not a Property: How Event Representations Create Blind Spots for Timing Attacks in Event-Based Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36386)
*Shoaib Ahmed Dipu,Md. Shaown Miah,Kamrul Hasan,Sayeed Shafayet Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了“观察者相对隐身”概念，利用事件相机数据中时间戳重排在不改变特定累积张量的前提下实现攻击。通过构造Null和C-PGD两种攻击方法，证明了针对特定观察者的完美隐身扰动在更精细或不同的时间视图下会暴露，且隐身性取决于扰动与观察者的关系而非扰动本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机的异步流特性使得同一时间戳变化对不同时间分辨率的下游模型（如粗粒度表示 vs 细粒度时序模型）产生不同影响。旨在揭示这种依赖性，即“观察者相对隐身”，并探索在严格限制可见性的情况下对事件视觉模型进行对抗攻击的可能性。

Method: 1. **理论定义**：定义“观察者相对隐身”和“受保护累积窗口内的精确盲空间”。2. **攻击构造**：提出梯度引导的时间戳重排攻击算法Null，以及基于约束投影梯度的优化器C-PGD。3. **评估指标**：定义SC-ASR_A(tau)以衡量在限制观察者A可见性的情况下的攻击成功率。4. **实验对比**：在DVS Gesture和DailyDVS-200数据集上，对比Null、C-PGD与其他五种攻击方法，并在不同观察者约束下进行消融实验。

Result: 1. **高成功率**：在DVS Gesture上，Null对ConvSNN达到81.56% ASR，对GRU达到98.67% ASR；在DailyDVS-200上达到99.28% ASR，且保持受保护张量完全不变。2. **唯一性优势**：在五种攻击比较中，Null是唯一在“精确观察者相等”条件下具有非零攻击成功率的方法。3. **C-PGD表现**：C-PGD同样实现了高ASR（DVS Gesture: 84.50%, DailyDVS-200: 89.55%）且保持精确隐身。4. **隐身脆弱性**：当引入观察者约束时，实值盲空间比例从87.5%降至62.5%，同时ConvSNN的攻击成功率显著下降（74.9% -> 37.2%）。

Conclusion: 事件视觉模型的对抗隐身性并非扰动的固有属性，而是依赖于扰动与特定观察者（处理机制）之间的关系。通过在受保护的累积窗口内重排时间戳，可以实现对特定模型的完美隐身攻击，但这些攻击在更精细或不同的时间视角下是可检测的。这强调了在设计鲁棒的事件视觉系统时需考虑时间处理的多样性。

Abstract: An event camera produces an asynchronous stream, but what is visible in that stream depends on how a downstream consumer, such as a model or detector, processes time. The same timestamp change may leave a coarse temporal representation unchanged while changing the response of a model that preserves finer timing. We characterize this dependence as observer-relative stealth. For recorded event streams, retiming an event within its protected accumulation window leaves the accumulated integer tensor exactly unchanged. We use this exact blind space to construct Null, a gradient-guided timestamp-retiming attack, and define SC-ASR_A(tau) to measure attack success while bounding the change visible to observer A. On DVS Gesture at a 10% event budget, Null reaches 81.56 +/- 5.81% ASR on ConvSNN and 98.67 +/- 0.45% on a GRU while preserving the protected tensor exactly. On DailyDVS-200, a protocol-scale Multi-View Fusion Network variant reaches 99.28 +/- 0.11% exact-null ASR, compared with 9.70 +/- 1.06% for its matched control. In a five-attack comparison, Null is the only method with nonzero attack success at exact observer equality, reaching 81.4% on DVS Gesture and 87.35% on DailyDVS-200. We also search the same exact blind space with an independently implemented constrained projected-gradient optimizer, C-PGD. At matched victim-gradient evaluations, C-PGD reaches 84.50 +/- 2.89% ASR on DVS Gesture and 89.55 +/- 4.39% on DailyDVS-200, again with exact protected equality. Perturbations that are exactly hidden from the protected observer become visible under shifted, finer, overlapping, and randomized temporal views. Adding observer constraints reduces the real-valued blind-space fraction from 87.5% to 75.0% to 62.5%, while DVS ConvSNN ASR falls from 74.9% to 61.9% to 37.2%. These results show that stealth is not a property of the perturbation alone.

</details>


### [27] [What Makes High-Magnification Knowledge Transferable? A Study of Cross-Resolution Distillation in Whole-Slide Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36407)
*Zhiyuan Yang,Jiahao Cheng,Mahdi S. Hosseini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过分解分析揭示了跨分辨率知识蒸馏中教师访问、表示损失和模型冗余的影响，发现直接预测虽重建误差低但无法充分捕捉教师目标的变化，且更好的重建并不必然提升下游性能，挑战了仅以重建误差衡量转移有效性的观点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨分辨率知识蒸馏旨在通过将高倍率表示转移到低倍率全切片分析来提升性能，但其有效转移的条件尚不明确。

Method: 开发基于分解的分析方法，探究三个核心问题：(a) 教师目标是否有助于任务，(b) 低倍率学生能否预测这些目标，(c) 切片模型是否从这些预测中受益；通过在涵盖分类、分级和生存预测的十个病理队列中进行对照实验进行验证。

Result: 提供教师区域均值与原生低倍率特征结合，在所有十个队列中均提升了下游性能；直接预测比残差预测具有更低的重建误差，但预测的特征未能充分反映教师目标中的变化；更好的重建并未一致地提高下游分数，且即使预测的教师特征固定，保留原生特征仍会改变性能。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了教师表示重建与其下游价值实现之间的差距，挑战了将重建误差作为跨分辨率转移唯一度量标准的充分性，并为诊断转移失效环节提供了框架，指出未来的蒸馏设计需同时考虑学生的预测能力和切片模型对预测特征的使用方式。

Abstract: Cross-resolution knowledge distillation aims to improve low-magnification whole- slide analysis by transferring high-magnification representations, yet the conditions for useful transfer remain unclear. We develop a decomposition-based analysis of teacher access, representation loss, and model excess, motivating three questions: whether (a) teacher targets help the task, (b) low-magnification students can predict them, and (c) slide models benefit from those predictions. We investigate them through controlled experiments across ten pathology cohorts spanning classifi- cation, grading, and survival prediction. In the main comparison, providing teacher regional means alongside native low-magnification features improves downstream performance in all ten cohorts. Direct prediction achieves lower reconstruction error than residual prediction, yet the predicted features underrepresent variation in the teacher targets. Moreover, better reconstruction does not consistently improve downstream scores, and retaining native features changes performance even when the predicted teacher features are held fixed. Together, these findings expose a gap between reconstructing teacher representations and realizing their downstream value. They challenge the sufficiency of reconstruction error as a measure of cross-resolution transfer and provide a diagnostic framework for examining where that transfer breaks down. Future distillation designs must account for both what students can predict and how slide models use those predictions.

</details>


### [28] [Towards Scalable Context-Aware Single-Cell Spatial Transcriptomics Prediction from Histology Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36429)
*Zijun Gao,Chunbin Gu,Jinxi Xiang,Xiangde Luo,Pheng-Ann Heng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CELLO框架，通过单次前向传播和网格采样实现从H&E图像到单细胞基因表达的高效预测，解决现有方法在分辨率和速度上的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于H&E图像的基因表达预测多在spot级别，掩盖了细胞异质性；直接应用病理基础模型存在尺度不匹配问题（混合多细胞或破坏形态），而纯分割模型缺乏强大的形态表征能力且受限于分割误差。

Method: 构建端到端框架CELLO：1) 对每张图像仅进行一次病理基础模型前向传播；2) 使用网格采样同时提取所有细胞的位置特异性特征；3) 引入距离衰减交叉注意力模块，利用空间偏置的局部形态上下文细化每个细胞的表示。

Result: 在包含12种器官、约1000万个细胞的52对Xenium-H&E数据上，CELLO提高了平均预测精度，并将全切片推理时间相比DeepSpot2Cell平均加速14.0倍（不含上游细胞分割）。

Conclusion: CELLO建立了从H&E图像进行单细胞基因表达预测的可扩展基础，兼顾了高精度与高效率。

Abstract: Predicting gene expression from H&E-stained histology images offers a scalable alternative to costly spatial transcriptomics, yet most existing methods operate at the spot level, where signals from multiple cells are aggregated and critical cellular heterogeneity is obscured. Extending this paradigm to single-cell resolution is non-trivial. Naively applying pathology foundation models faces a scale mismatch: their patch-level representations mix multiple cells, whereas per-cell cropping or resizing distorts morphology and removes local context. Conversely, segmentation-based models without strong pretrained visual encoders often lack the morphological representation capacity needed for accurate molecular prediction and inherit errors from imperfect cell boundary masks. Here, we present CELLO, an efficient end-to-end framework that performs a single pathology foundation model forward pass per image and uses grid sampling to extract location-specific features for all cells simultaneously. We further introduce a distance-decay cross-attention module that refines each cell representation using spatially biased local morphological context. Using 52 public Xenium-H&E pairs from HEST-1k that span 12 organs and approximately 10 million cells, CELLO improves the average predictive accuracy over the evaluated baselines while reducing the mean whole-slide inference time compared to DeepSpot2Cell, a 14.0x speed-up on average that excludes upstream cell segmentation. Our work establishes a scalable foundation for single-cell gene expression prediction from H&E images.

</details>


### [29] [Temporal-Aware Fusion for Robust Outdoor LiDAR Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36432)
*Minghang Zhu,Zhijing Wang,Yuxin Guo,Chen Liu,Yongshu Huang,Wen Li,Sheng Ao,Cheng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TempLoc框架，通过建模时序一致性解决LiDAR重定位在动态/模糊场景下的局限，显著提升室外定位鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于回归的方法多侧重单帧推理，未能充分利用扫描间的时空一致性，导致在动态或模糊场景中表现受限。

Method: 构建全局坐标估计模块预测点级坐标及不确定性；利用注意力机制进行先验坐标生成以估计帧间对应关系；部署不确定性引导的坐标融合模块，端到端整合对应关系预测。

Result: 在NCLT和Oxford RobotCar基准测试中，TempLoc大幅优于最先进方法，验证了时序感知对应建模的有效性。

Conclusion: 有效建模序列一致性可显著增强LiDAR重定位的鲁棒性与精度。

Abstract: LiDAR relocalization aims to estimate the global 6-DoF pose of a sensor in the environment. However, existing regression-based approaches often encounter limitations in dynamic or ambiguous scenarios, as they typically prioritize single-frame inference, leaving the potential of spatio-temporal consistency across scans not fully explored. In this paper, we propose a Temporal-aware Localization framework (TempLoc) designed to enhance the robustness of outdoor localization by effectively modeling sequential consistency. Specifically, a Global Coordinate Estimation module is first introduced to predict point-wise global coordinates and associated uncertainties for each LiDAR scan. A Prior Coordinate Generation module is then presented to estimate inter-frame point correspondences by the attention mechanism. Lastly, an Uncertainty-Guided Coordinate Fusion module is deployed to integrate both predictions of point correspondence in an end-to-end fashion, yielding a more temporally consistent and accurate global 6-DoF pose. Experimental results on the NCLT and Oxford RobotCar benchmarks show that our TempLoc outperforms state-of-the-art methods by a large margin, demonstrating the effectiveness of temporal-aware correspondence modeling in LiDAR relocalization.

</details>


### [30] [RA-CFGCache: From Branch-Level Criteria to Guided-Risk Control under Classifier-Free Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36433)
*Yiming Liu,Ben Wan,Tongxuan Liu,Ao Wang,Yuqi Xiong,Fan Zhang,Hui Chen,Guiguang Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RA-CFGCache框架，通过风险对齐解决CFG下缓存控制的两类不匹配问题（分支引导不匹配和局部-最终不匹配），结合CFG系数校准分支误差组合及时间步传播先验，在保持采样调度不变的前提下显著优化效率与保真度权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在分类器无关引导（CFG）下的迭代去噪计算成本高；现有免训练缓存方法未明确考虑CFG下缓存误差的组合方式及局部扰动对最终输出的影响，导致缓存控制存在偏差。

Method: 提出RA-CFGCache框架：1) CFG-aware Guided-Risk Composition：利用CFG系数和离线校准的跨分支对齐信息组合分支级代理；2) Propagation-Aware Rescaling：根据从孤立重用扰动校准的时间步相关传播先验加权有向风险估计；3) 在线阈值控制器决定双分支联合刷新或重用。

Result: 在FLUX.1-dev、Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B和CogVideoX-2B上实验表明，该方法优于现有免训练缓存基线，实现更优的效率-保真度权衡；兼容TeaCache、DiCache、MagCache等多种基础代理族，在几乎不增加延迟的情况下持续提升保真度。

Conclusion: RA-CFGCache通过显式建模CFG下的误差组合与传播特性，有效解决了现有缓存方法的局限性，为高效高质量视觉生成提供了通用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Diffusion models enable high-quality visual generation, but iterative denoising remains computationally expensive, especially under classifier-free guidance (CFG), which requires both conditional and unconditional evaluations. Training-free caching reduces this cost by reuse of previously computed features or predictions. However, existing branch-local reuse criteria do not explicitly account for how cache errors combine under CFG or how local perturbations affect the final output. We identify two misalignments in cache control: a branch-guided mismatch, where guided error depends on both the magnitudes and alignment of branch errors, and a local-final mismatch, where the downstream impact of a local error varies across timesteps. We propose RA-CFGCache, a Risk-Aligned Caching framework under CFG that incorporates both factors while keeping the sampling schedule and guidance rule fixed. CFG-aware Guided-Risk Composition combines existing branch-wise proxies using CFG coefficients and offline-calibrated cross-branch alignment. Propagation-Aware Rescaling further weights the resulting guided-risk estimate with a timestep-dependent propagation prior calibrated from isolated reuse perturbations. An online threshold controller then determines when to jointly refresh or reuse both branches. Experiments on FLUX.1-dev, Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B, and CogVideoX-2B demonstrate improved efficiency--fidelity trade-offs over evaluated training-free caching baselines. Moreover, RA-CFGCache is compatible with diverse base proxy families, including TeaCache-, DiCache-, and MagCache-style estimators, and consistently improves fidelity at nearly unchanged latency. Code is available at https://github.com/yiming-l21/RA-CFGCache.git.

</details>


### [31] [Merlin Plus: A Large-Scale, Multi-Cancer, Image-Mask-Report Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36436)
*Pedro R. A. S. Bassi,Wenxuan Li,Szymon Plotka,Ruby Honjol,Jakub Przado,Xinze Zhou,Kang Wang,Yang Yang,Malte Jensen,Akshay S. Chaudhari,Curtis P. Langlotz,Alan L. Yuille,Zongwei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 介绍首个大规模CT多器官肿瘤掩码数据集Merlin Plus，通过报告驱动的主动学习框架生成高质量标注，支持多癌种分割与纵向分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决CT影像中跨不同器官的多癌种分割因缺乏肿瘤掩码数据而面临的根本性限制，突破标注数据稀缺的瓶颈。

Method: 扩展原有Merlin数据集，构建包含9个器官、1,153个逐体素肿瘤掩码及纵向元数据的Merlin Plus数据集；开发基于放射学报告的主动学习框架，利用报告定位病例，由模型生成初始掩码并经放射科医生审核修正，以降低标注负担并保证质量。

Result: 成功构建了高质量的大规模多器官CT肿瘤标注数据集，实现了从报告到精细掩码的高效转化，并提供了支持癌症进展时间建模的纵向数据。

Conclusion: Merlin Plus通过直接解决多癌种分割掩码稀缺问题，为可扩展的多器官癌症检测、分割及纵向CT分析提供了关键数据基础。

Abstract: Multi-cancer segmentation in computed tomography (CT) is fundamentally limited by the scarcity of tumor masks across different organs. We present Merlin Plus, the first large-scale CT dataset with radiologist-created tumor masks across 9 organs. Merlin Plus extends the Merlin dataset by adding 1,153 per-voxel tumor masks and longitudinal metadata. To create these tumor masks, we developed a report-based active-learning framework in which radiology reports identify tumor cases for annotation and support training of a tumor segmentation model. The model generates initial masks, which radiologists review and correct to produce the final masks, reducing annotation burden while maintaining high-quality annotations. Besides tumor masks, the longitudinal metadata in Merlin Plus enables temporal modeling of cancer progression. By directly addressing the major bottleneck of limited multi-cancer segmentation masks, Merlin Plus supports scalable multi-organ cancer detection, segmentation, and longitudinal analysis in CT. Dataset is available at: https://github.com/MrGiovanni/MerlinPlus

</details>


### [32] [DARE to Mitigate Hallucination: Dual-path Auto-Regressive-aware Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36440)
*Jae-Ho Lee,Jeong-Eun Lee,Gyeong-Moon Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DARE框架，通过结合文本对比、自回归感知信号及图像对比，解决大视觉语言模型中的物体幻觉问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于教师强制（TF）对比的无训练编辑方法可能无法充分反映自回归解码过程中的生成动态和多模态交互，导致幻觉抑制效果受限。

Method: 提出DARE（Dual-path Auto-Regressive-aware Editing），构建混合幻觉编辑方向：(1)基于TF的文本对比；(2)解码期间的自回归感知表示转换；(3)配对图像的受控视觉差异。

Result: 在多个LVLM幻觉基准测试中，DARE持续减少物体幻觉，同时保持多模态感知能力和推理效率。

Conclusion: DARE通过整合互补的对比路径和自回归感知信号，有效解决了传统TF方法在捕捉生成动态方面的不足，提升了幻觉抑制性能。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) have recently achieved remarkable progress across multimodal tasks, yet object hallucination remains a persistent challenge where models generate descriptions inconsistent with the visual input. Recent work mitigates hallucinations through training-free representation editing, typically by constructing hallucination-related directions from teacher-forcing (TF) contrasts between hallucinated and truthful responses. However, LVLMs operate through autoregressive (AR) decoding during generation, raising the question of whether TF-based analysis fully reflects the generation dynamics that lead to hallucinated outputs. In this paper, we analyze the relationship between TF-based editing and AR generation behavior and find that TF-based editing alone may be insufficient to capture both decoding dynamics and multimodal interactions associated with hallucinations. To address this limitation, we propose DARE (Dual-path Auto-Regressive-aware Editing), a hybrid hallucination editing framework that integrates two complementary contrast pathways: textual contrasts and image contrasts, together with autoregressive-aware representation signals. Specifically, DARE constructs hallucination editing directions from (1) TF-based textual contrasts, (2) AR-aware representation transitions during decoding, and (3) controlled visual differences between paired images. Extensive experiments on multiple LVLM hallucination benchmarks demonstrate that DARE consistently reduces object hallucinations while preserving multimodal perception capability and inference efficiency. Our implementation code is available at https://github.com/KU-VGI/DARE.

</details>


### [33] [Online Versatile Incremental Learning: Towards Class and Domain-Agnostic Adaptation at Any Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36442)
*Jae-Ho Lee,Min-Yeong Park,Jun-Yeong Moon,Jung Uk Kim,Gyeong-Moon Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出在线通用增量学习（Online VIL）新场景及TopFlow框架，通过域无关流匹配和全局拓扑保持机制，解决类别与视觉域同步动态变化的持续学习难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法无法捕捉类别和领域同时发生的连续且并发的变化，而真实世界部署需要适应这种无明确边界的动态环境。

Method: 提出TopFlow框架，包含两个互补机制：1) 域无关流匹配（DFM），将测地线流核集成到对比学习中以获取域无关表示；2) 全局拓扑保持（GTP），在不显式存储过去样本的情况下维持特征空间的全局结构。

Result: 在具有挑战性的Online VIL场景中，TopFlow有效解决了现有方法的局限性，取得了最先进的性能表现。

Conclusion: 该方法为构建适应真实动态环境的持续学习系统提供了潜在方向，证明了在处理类与域同步演变方面的有效性。

Abstract: Continual learning enables vision systems to adapt to ever-changing data distributions. Despite significant advances, existing approaches fail to capture continuous and concurrent shifts in classes and domains, a critical capability for real-world deployment. This work introduces Online VIL (Online Versatile Incremental Learning), a novel scenario where class concepts and visual domains evolve simultaneously online without explicit boundaries. To better adapt to the challenges of such dynamic environments that more closely resemble real-world conditions, we propose a novel framework TopFlow, Topology preservation with Flow matching representation that contains two complementary mechanisms: Domain-agnostic Flow Matching (DFM) and Global Topology Preservation (GTP). DFM guides the model to have domain-agnostic representations by integrating the geodesic flow kernel into contrastive learning. In contrast, GTP maintains the global structure of the feature space without explicitly storing past examples. Our extensive experiments demonstrate that TopFlow effectively addresses the limitations of existing methods within the Online VIL scenario, achieving state-of-the-art performance in challenging Online VIL. The proposed methods suggest potential directions for building continual learning systems in realistic dynamic environments. Our implementation code is available at https://github.com/KU-VGI/Online-VIL.

</details>


### [34] [DynamicHOI: Coupled Dynamics for Physics-aware HOI Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36454)
*Wenliang Guo,Zhanbo Huang,Yu Kong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DynamicHOI框架，通过结合几何扩散细化与耦合的手-物动力学，解决单目RGB视频中手部-物体交互（HOI）重建中视觉合理但机械不一致的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要依赖视觉和几何一致性约束，导致重建轨迹在物理动力学层面缺乏充分约束，产生机械上不合理的手部运动。

Method: 1. 使用基于几何的扩散模型进行轨迹细化；2. 利用逆动力学和牛顿-欧拉方程计算手部广义力和物体受力，提供动力学级监督；3. 通过接触力传递耦合手与物的动力学，并将主动手部驱动建模为概率先验以抑制不合理的运动。

Result: 在三个HOI数据集上，手部和物体的重建精度均得到一致提升。

Conclusion: 引入物理感知的HOI建模不仅提升了重建质量，还显著改善了下游应用（如手部世界模型生成和机器人操作学习）的效果，证明了其超越单纯重建的价值。

Abstract: We study hand-object interaction (HOI) reconstruction from monocular RGB videos, where partial observations can produce visually plausible yet mechanically inconsistent trajectories. Existing methods mainly enforce visual and geometric agreement, leaving the underlying interaction dynamics insufficiently constrained. We propose DynamicHOI, a physics-aware HOI reconstruction framework combining geometry-grounded diffusion refinement with coupled hand-object dynamics. Geometry spatially grounds visual evidence for trajectory refinement, while articulated inverse dynamics and Newton-Euler dynamics derive hand generalized forces and object wrenches for dynamics-level supervision. We further couple hand and object dynamics through contact-force transfer and recover active hand actuation as an interaction-level physical quantity. We formulate its empirical magnitude distribution into a probabilistic prior that penalizes unlikely actuation and suppresses mechanically implausible reconstructed motion. Experiments on three HOI datasets show consistent improvements in both hand and object reconstruction. The reconstructed trajectories further benefit downstream applications including hand world-model generation and robotic manipulation learning, demonstrating the value of physics-aware HOI modeling beyond reconstruction.

</details>
