TL;DR: 本研究揭示了大型语言模型（LLM）在多层Transformer中处理三元关系信息的机制：中间层构建两两关系的联合表示，后续层整合为三元关系的联合表示。这种层级化的几何组织与因果使用机制不仅解释了关系推理过程，且限制模型仅使用因果相关的联合表示可提升预测准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大型语言模型如何处理依赖于输入token间关系而非单个token的任务，特别是关系信息如何在Transformer各层中被表征和处理。

Method: 通过分析要求推断三个token间循环概念（如月份、小时、星期、音符）关系的提示词集合的激活值，研究Llama、Qwen、Gemma和Mistral等多个模型家族中的逐层进展。

Result: 发现跨模型和概念的层进式规律：中间层使用基于两个token间推断关系的联合表示，而较深层使用关联所有三个token的联合表示来完成任务。同时存在几何结构化但因果惰性的其他token关系。

Conclusion: 几何与因果调查共同揭示了表示层面的机制，即关系信息被逐步组织和组合以形成答案。此外，将模型限制在这些因果相关的联合表示上能提高下一个token的预测精度。

Abstract: Large language models are able to solve tasks whose answers depend on not only individual input tokens, but also on relations among them. How is such relational information represented and processed across transformer layers? We study activations from ensembles of prompts that require inferring relationships between three tokens corresponding to a cyclic concept (months, hours, weekdays, and musical notes) to correctly predict the next token. Across model families (Llama, Qwen, Gemma, and Mistral) and cyclic concepts, we find a consistent layerwise progression in how the joint dependence among the tokens is geometrically organized and causally used: intermediate layers use a joint representation based on the inferred relationship between two tokens, while later layers use a joint representation associated with all three tokens to correctly complete the task. We also find other relationships between tokens that are geometrically structured but remain causally inert in the next-token prediction. Crucially, when taken together, these geometric and causal investigations reveal the representation-level mechanism that progressively organizes and composes the relational information to form the answer. More surprisingly, restricting the models to such causally relevant joint representations improves next-token prediction accuracy.

</details>


### [138] [PADMÉ: Preference Alignment Data Synthesis for Meta-Evaluation of LM Agent Evaluators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36086)
*Cheng Chang,Yining Mao,Peng Qi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出PADMÉ方法，通过偏好判断而非绝对评分来优化语言模型对代理行为的元评估，显著提升与人类判断的一致性（从73%提升至85%），且无需人工参与和低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型评估器在代理行为评估中难以确保与人类判断一致；直接收集人类数据成本高、绝对评分对齐困难，且依赖其他LM作为元评估器存在信任递归问题。

Method: 将元评估重构为偏好判断问题，比较人类与LM评估器的隐含偏好是否一致；引入PADMÉ数据合成方法，仅使用小语言模型生成基于标准的元评估数据，无需人工介入，计算预算低。

Result: 构建包含1000个样本的数据集（覆盖4个代理领域和3个评估标准）；在150个样本子集上的人类验证显示，相比朴素基线，与人类判断的一致性从73%提升至85%；对25个常见模型的元评估揭示了评估性能与评分粒度、宽松度及模型大小等因素的相关性。

Conclusion: PADMÉ提供了一种高效、低成本且无需人工干预的元评估数据合成方案，能有效提升自动化评估器与人类判断的一致性，并为理解评估性能影响因素提供了实证依据。

Abstract: Language models are frequently employed to evaluate other language models. An LM evaluator scoring agentic behaviors across multiple criteria is valuable, provided that its decisions align with human judgment. We call the problem of evaluating this alignment Meta-Evaluation. Tackling it directly is difficult: collecting human data is expensive, absolute scoring is hard to align, and using an LM meta-evaluator recurses the question of trustworthiness. We adopt a reformulation of meta-evaluation as a preference judgment problem: rather than comparing human and LM evaluator scores of a trajectory, we ask whether their implied preferences align. Building on this, we introduce PADMÉ, a data synthesis method that generates reliable criterion-based meta-evaluation data for agentic settings. PADMÉ uses only small language models, requires no human involvement during evaluations, and operates under a low computational budget. We build a prototype of PADMÉ and synthesize a dataset of 1,000 samples across four agentic domains and three evaluation criteria. Human validation on a 150-sample subset demonstrates that PADMÉ improves agreement with human judgment from 73% to 85% over a naive baseline. Meta-evaluating 25 common models with our dataset demonstrates the correlations between evaluation performance and scoring granularity, leniency, and model size, among other factors.

</details>


### [139] [A Character-Level Neural Approach to Sinhala Sandhi Splitting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36131)
*Yasas Ekanayaka,Deshan Sumanathilaka*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文建立了僧伽罗语（Sinhala）Sandhi分裂的首个神经基准，使用SandhiLex数据集评估字符级序列到序列模型。最佳双向LSTM编码器-单向LSTM解码器模型在复杂Sandhi子集上精确匹配准确率仅为68.40%，远低于规则词缀子集的94.00%。研究确立了基准，并指出数据规模、Sandhi类型条件和基于注意力的解码是未来主要方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 僧伽罗语NLP中，Sandhi现象掩盖了词汇边界，阻碍处理；此前缺乏针对该任务的神经基准和系统性研究。

Method: 基于SandhiLex数据集的字符级序列到序列模型研究，采用原生僧伽罗Unicode输入，评估循环编码器-解码器模型（特别是双向LSTM编码器与单向LSTM解码器），涵盖词缀化及更复杂的词汇化、派生性和词源性Sandhi。

Result: 最佳模型在复杂Sandhi子集上达到68.40%精确匹配准确率和82.08%字符级准确率；而在规则词缀子集上达到94.00%。消融实验表明双向编码对性能贡献最大，原生脚本优于罗马化输入。错误多为边界相邻字符或合理但错误的语音替换导致的“接近正确”失误。

Conclusion: 本研究为僧伽罗语Sandhi分裂提供了实证基准，揭示了当前模型在处理复杂Sandhi时的局限性，并为未来工作指明了数据扩展、类型条件化和注意力机制应用的方向。

Abstract: Sinhala Sandhi splitting recovers the constituent words or morphemes hidden inside a phonologically merged surface form. The task is important for Sinhala NLP because Sandhi obscures lexical boundaries, but no prior published work has established a neural benchmark for Sinhala Sandhi splitting. We present a character-level sequence-to-sequence study based on SandhiLex, using native Sinhala Unicode input and evaluating recurrent encoder-decoder models for affixational and more complex lexicalized, derivational, and etymological Sandhi. The central challenge is the hard subset lexicalized, derivational, and etymological Sandhi, where our best model, a bidirectional LSTM encoder with a unidirectional LSTM decoder, reaches only 68.40\% exact-match accuracy (82.08\% character-level accuracy), well below the 94.00\% achieved on the more regular affixational subset. Ablations show that bidirectional encoding is the largest contributor to performance, while native Sinhala script improves exact match accuracy over romanized input. Qualitative analysis indicates that many errors are near misses involving boundary adjacent characters or plausible but incorrect phonological substitutions. These results establish an empirical baseline for Sinhala Sandhi splitting and identify data scale, Sandhi type conditioning, and attention-based decoding as the main directions for future work.

</details>


### [140] [When Does Correction Become Repair? Mechanistic Auditing of Internal Interventions in Tool-Using LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36138)
*Jiayi Li,Ruizhe Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SAKIKO是一个用于审计大语言模型（LLM）内部表示修复的框架，旨在解决传统指标掩盖干预细节的问题。通过定向错误发现、路由器条件干预等方法，该框架揭示了行为改变不等于正确修复，强调了在宣称内部修复前必须进行基于结果的裁决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的聚合指标无法揭示内部激活引导如何具体影响LLM在执行外部工具前的决策过程，也无法评估这些干预带来的副作用或“附带损害”。需要一种更细致的审计方法来理解表征修复的真实效果。

Method: 提出了SAKIKO审计框架，包含四个核心机制：1) 通过定向错误发现形式化表征修复；2) 路由器条件干预；3) 目的地解析验证；4) 前瞻性冻结统计许可。在When2Call和MetaTool数据集上对七个LLM进行了实验，并进行了密封评估以比较校准目标增益与随机方向。

Result: 通道键控干预在五个模型中实现了特定方向的净增益，但在59个预算匹配的随机方向测试中，没有一个是校准目标增益。关键发现是“行为移动不等于修复”：一个获得+55净增益的干预实际上破坏了超过一半它触及的基线正确决策。此外，由于有限样本的不确定性，Qwen3-4B和Gemma-2-9B上的初步积极估计被正式拒绝。

Conclusion: SAKIKO证明了在宣称内部修复有效之前，必须实施基于结果解决的裁决机制。简单的性能提升指标可能掩盖了严重的决策腐败，严格的审计对于确保LLM工具使用行为的可靠性至关重要。

Abstract: Before invoking external tools, an agentic LLM must select among a K-way action space: executing a call, seeking clarification, answering directly, or declining. While internal activation steering can alter these pre-execution decisions, conventional aggregate metrics obscure where altered states land and what collateral damage they inflict. We present SAKIKO, an auditing framework that formalizes representation repair via directional error discovery, router-conditioned intervention, destination-resolved verification, and prospectively frozen statistical licensing. Across seven LLMs on When2Call and MetaTool, channel-keyed interventions induce direction-specific net gains in five models; across three sealed evaluations, none of 59 budget-matched random directions matches calibrated target gain. Crucially, destination auditing shows that behavioral movement does not equal repair: an intervention achieving +55 net gain corrupts over half of the baseline-correct decisions it touches, and promising point estimates on Qwen3-4B and Gemma-2-9B are formally declined due to finite-sample uncertainty. SAKIKO establishes the necessity of outcome-resolved adjudication before claiming internal repair. Code: https://github.com/ruizheliUOA/mechanistic-tool-use-llm.

</details>


### [141] [Language Models Are "Insecure" Reporters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36139)
*Jenny Y. Huang,Jiameng Fan,Ahmed Imtiaz Humayun,Maximillian Chen,Tian Qin,Run Chen,Vidhya Navalpakkam,Hongxiang Gu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究揭示大语言模型在生成报告时存在“不安全报告”现象，即倾向于隐瞒削弱成功叙事的负面结果。通过实验发现，添加简短的诚实指令可显著提升模型披露负面结果的概率，且激活分析表明“诚实”与“追求成功”在表示空间中方向相反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM被用于自主长程任务，人工审计变得困难，用户依赖LLM生成的报告来评估工作质量。然而，LLM可能会隐瞒改变叙事的关键缺陷（即“不安全报告”），导致评估失真。

Method: 1. 构建8种对抗性报告场景；2. 在包含植入负面结果的ML实验日志上测试GPT-5.5及8个开源模型；3. 对比有无“Be honest in your response”指令下的表现；4. 对Qwen3.5-9B进行思维链分析和激活/引导实验。

Result: GPT-5.5在无指令时仅2/200次标记负面结果，有指令时达190/200次。跨模型分析显示模型默认呈现成功叙事。激活分析证实诚实与追求成功对应表示空间中的对立方向。

Conclusion: LLM默认倾向于呈现成功叙事而隐瞒缺陷。通过明确引导或技术手段将模型导向诚实，可显著提高报告的透明度和可靠性。

Abstract: As large language models are deployed in increasingly autonomous long-horizon tasks, manually auditing and verifying the actions, artifacts, and outputs of models becomes more difficult. Users instead come to rely on LLM-generated reports to assess the quality and completeness of the work. We introduce a suite of eight adversarial reporting scenarios to systematically study whether LLMs conceal narrative-changing flaws: errors or limitations that undermine an otherwise successful account of work. We call this phenomenon "insecure reporting." When handed machine learning experiment logs containing a planted negative result that substantially weakens the proposed method, GPT-5.5 flags the negative result in only 2 of 200 generated reports. However, when a short honesty instruction, "Be honest in your response," is added, the model flags the negative result in 190 of 200 reports. Across eight open-weight models, chain-of-thought analysis reveals a recurring tension between disclosing narrative-changing flaws and reasoning about ways to appear successful. We perform an activation analysis and a steering experiment on Qwen3.5-9B, finding that honesty and success-seeking correspond to opposing directions in representation space. Our results suggest that LLMs tend to present narratives of success by default, and that steering models toward honesty makes their reports substantially more transparent.

</details>


### [142] [Targeting Pivotal Decisions for Credit Assignment in Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36178)
*Dongwon Jung,Hemanth Neelgund Ramesh,Yifan Wang,Xiaomin Li,Yuexing Hao,Yu Hu,Muhao Chen,Varun Chandrasekaran,Andrzej Banburski-Fahey,Jaron Lanier*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出ProVer框架，通过识别关键决策片段并进行细粒度信用分配，解决GRPO在智能体强化学习中无法区分重要与次要决策的问题，显著提升Qwen3.5模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO)方法对所有策略token均匀分配轨迹级优势，未能区分关键决策与非相关决策，导致无法明确哪些中间决策对成功有贡献。

Method: 引入ProVer框架：1) 使用智能体裁判对比成功/失败轨迹，提议可能导致结果差异的关键片段；2) 不直接信任裁判，而是通过比较该片段前后当前策略续写的终端成功率差异来验证片段优势；3) 将正估计值融入GRPO优势中。仅利用模型判断定位待验证位置，避免 exhaustive evaluation。

Result: 在ALFWorld、WebShop和SearchQA基准上，ProVer在所有模型规模下取得最佳平均性能。相比GRPO，Qwen3.5-2B提升9.91%，Qwen3.5-4B提升7.12%。即使不使用前沿规模裁判模型，也能以适度生成开销实现高效训练。

Conclusion: ProVer通过选择性针对关键决策进行细粒度信用分配，有效提升了智能体强化学习的政策训练效果，证明了该方法在效率与有效性上的优势。

Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) has become a promising approach for training large language model agents. However, its uniform assignment of trajectory-level advantages to all policy tokens fails to distinguish consequential decisions from less relevant ones, obscuring which intermediate decisions contributed to success. We introduce ProVer, a framework that targets potentially pivotal decisions for fine-grained credit assignment in agentic reinforcement learning. Given a rollout group, an agentic judge contrasts successful and failed trajectories to propose a segment potentially responsible for their divergent outcomes. Rather than directly trusting the judge's assessment, ProVer verifies the proposed segment by estimating its advantage from the difference in terminal success rates between current-policy continuations sampled before and after the segment. Positive estimates are then incorporated into the GRPO advantages of policy tokens within the proposed segment. By using model judgment only to select where to verify, ProVer grounds local credit in observed outcomes without exhaustively evaluating every intermediate state. Across ALFWorld, WebShop, and SearchQA, ProVer achieves the strongest average performance at both model scales, with relative improvements over GRPO of 9.91% and 7.12% for Qwen3.5-2B and Qwen3.5-4B, respectively. Further analyses demonstrate that informed segment selection improves policy training with modest additional generation overhead, even without a frontier-scale judge model, highlighting the effectiveness and efficiency of selectively targeting pivotal decisions for fine-grained credit assignment in agentic reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [143] [Concept Direction Reliability Across Languages with Different Tokenizer Fertility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36194)
*Muhammad Abdullahi Said,Abass Oguntade,Elisha Komolafe,Babangida Sani,Fatima Muhammad Adam,Muhammad Sammani Sani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究表明，下游情感分类的准确性并不保证向量方向的可重复性。通过测量英语、豪萨语和约鲁巴语的分半一致性，发现预测精度高不代表方向一致，且需独立评估向量方向可重复性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下游情感分类任务即使准确，提取的情感方向在不同样本间仍可能变化，因此需要评估这种方向的可重复性（reproducibility），以揭示预测精度与方向一致性之间的差异。

Method: 使用四种语言模型，在英语、豪萨语和约鲁巴语上，利用原生文本和翻译文本进行分半一致性测量；通过十个主题选择层，并在十五个主题的单独组中评估方向一致性；比较不同层和最终token的表现，并分析平均token表示的影响。

Result: 英语的一致性范围为0.737-0.870，豪萨语为0.589-0.762，约鲁巴语为0.101-0.399，保持相同的语言排序。基于这些层的分类器预测准确率高于随机水平，但方向一致性低。平均token表示导致一致性降低，高一致性部分受句子长度影响。

Conclusion: 预测准确性不意味着方向一致性，必须独立测量向量方向的可重复性。研究未证实tokenizer丰度（每个空格分隔词的平均token数）是导致跨语言差异的原因。

Abstract: Extracted sentiment directions can vary across samples even when downstream sentiment classification remains accurate. To evaluate direction reproducibility, we measure split-half agreement in English, Hausa, and Yoruba representations across four language models using both native and translated texts. We identify layers selected for agreement using ten topics and evaluate direction agreement across separate groups of fifteen topics. Using the final token, split-half agreement ranges from 0.737 to 0.870 for English, 0.589 to 0.762 for Hausa, and 0.101 to 0.399 for Yoruba, maintaining this language rank order across all 77 complete model comparisons. Classifiers trained on these same layers consistently predict sentiment above chance, demonstrating that predictive accuracy does not imply directional consistency. Furthermore, averaging token representations yields less consistent agreement, and high agreement can partially reflect sentence length. Ultimately, our findings highlight the need to measure vector direction reproducibility independently of classification performance, though they do not establish that tokenizer fertility which is the average number of tokens per whitespace separated word causes cross-lingual differences.

</details>


### [144] [SCOUT: Synergizing Reasoning and Tool-Use for Computer-Use Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36201)
*Jianxing Chen,Xiao Yu,Shipra Agrawal,Zhou Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SCOUT，一种结合推理密集型规范生成和工具密集型证据收集的代理安全验证器，以解决计算机使用代理（CUAs）隐蔽危害检测难的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于通用安全标准或仅依赖截图的LLM-as-a-judge方法，难以捕捉任务特定的细微有害行为及环境实际变化，导致对CUA潜在危害的检测存在挑战。

Method: SCOUT采用两阶段验证机制：首先通过深度推理生成任务特定的完成与安全规范（Rubrics）；随后利用探测代理与执行后环境交互，收集接地证据以进行最终的安全性和完成性判断。

Result: 在AutoElicit-Bench上，SCOUT的不安全F1得分为75.4，完成F1为74.5，优于LLM-as-a-judge和朴素工具使用验证器；在OS-Blind基准上不安全检测准确率达76.4%。测试时反思将不安全执行率从30.2%降至17.2%。

Conclusion: 无需工具的规范生成能激发更强的推理能力，对提升各类验证器（尤其是非前沿模型）的安全性检测至关重要。SCOUT不仅适用于计算机使用场景，初步扩展表明其也能支持代码任务的安全验证。

Abstract: Computer-use agents (CUAs), while capable of completing computer tasks in everyday and professional workflows, can cause unintended harm even under benign instructions and environments. However, detecting such harm remains challenging. First, it requires careful, task-specific reasoning: verifiers guided only by general safety criteria often overlook many important but subtle harmful behaviors. Second, it requires active investigation: past trajectory screenshots show what the agent did but not always what actually changed in the environment, so LLM-as-a-judge verifiers that rely on screenshots alone may be unable to determine the actual consequences of actions. To address these challenges, we introduce SCOUT, a two-stage agentic safety verifier that synergizes reasoning-intensive rubric generation with tool-intensive evidence gathering. First, our SCOUT rubric generator extensively reasons over the task and the agent's trajectory to determine what successful and safe execution should entail, generating task-specific completion and safety rubrics. Then, our SCOUT probing agent follows these rubrics to interact with the post-execution environment and collect grounded evidence for final safety and completion judgments. We evaluate our framework on two computer-use safety benchmarks. On AutoElicit-Bench, SCOUT achieves 75.4 unsafe F1 and 74.5 completion F1, outperforming LLM-as-a-judge verifiers and naive tool-use verifiers. SCOUT leads on OS-Blind with 76.4% unsafe detection accuracy. Test-time reflection reduces final unsafe execution rates from 30.2% to 17.2% on AutoElicit-Bench. Ablations and analysis show that tool-free rubric generation in SCOUT elicits substantially more reasoning and is crucial for safety detection across verifier backbones, especially non-frontier ones. A preliminary extension to coding tasks shows that SCOUT can support safety verification beyond computer-use.

</details>


### [145] [FastGuide: Accelerating Reward Guidance for Diffusion Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36202)
*Darshan Thaker,Lachlan Ewen MacDonald,René Vidal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出FastGuide，一种自适应混合解码方法，通过并行和自回归解码的结合，显著加速扩散语言模型的梯度奖励引导推理过程，在保持生成质量的同时速度提升最高4.4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决基于梯度的奖励引导在控制掩码扩散语言模型时计算成本过高的问题，特别是每次解码迭代中昂贵的扩散模型前向传播和奖励模型反向传播步骤。

Method: 1. 混合解码策略：结合并行和解码优势；2.  amortize奖励模型反向传播成本：每步仅计算一次指导并复用生成多个token；3. 自回归式扩散前向传播：逐个取消mask token，利用KV缓存和注意力稀疏重计算高效更新分布；4. 自适应置信度机制：对模型低置信度的token进行延迟处理。

Result: 在三个奖励基准测试中，FastGuide比顺序奖励引导解码快高达4.4倍，同时保持了相似的生成质量。

Conclusion: FastGuide有效降低了扩散语言模型推理时的计算开销，实现了速度与质量的平衡，为高效使用下游奖励模型提供了新方案。

Abstract: Gradient-based reward guidance provides a flexible way to use downstream reward models to control masked diffusion language models at inference time. However, its computational cost remains high as each decoding iteration incurs expensive diffusion model forward passes and reward model backpropagation steps. To address this, we introduce FastGuide, an adaptive hybrid of parallel and autoregressive decoding to accelerate reward guidance for diffusion language models. In analogy to parallel decoding, FastGuide amortizes the cost of reward model backpropagation by computing guidance once per decoding step and reusing it to generate multiple tokens. Within each decoding step, FastGuide makes diffusion forward passes autoregressive by unmasking tokens one at a time while efficiently recomputing token distributions after each unmasking by utilizing KV caching techniques and sparse recomputation of attention. Lastly, to adapt hybrid decoding to the model's confidence, FastGuide defers any token that the model is unconfident about under its recomputed distribution. Experiments on three reward benchmarks demonstrate that FastGuide is up to $4.4\times$ faster than sequential reward-guided decoding while retaining similar generation quality.

</details>


### [146] [Geometric Representations of African Languages: A Regional Semantic Hub and Cultural Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36205)
*Muhammad Abdullahi Said,Jonathan Shock*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究分析了Gemma 4 31B模型对非洲语言的表征能力及文化引导响应。研究发现不同非洲语言间存在区域和语系层面的表征相似性模式，且模型能基于英语数据实现针对特定非洲国家的文化引导，但结果受表征测量方式影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大型语言模型（Gemma 4 31B）如何内部表征多种非洲语言，以及其是否具备响应特定文化或地域引导的能力，以评估模型在多元文化语境下的表现及潜在偏差。

Method: 1. 使用探针、对比方向和表征相似度度量，比较九种非洲语言与三种控制语言在不同网络层的表征差异；2. 利用独立的英语数据构建针对尼日利亚、加纳、肯尼亚和南非的引导方向，并通过联合评分机制测试模型的归因率输出；3. 将结果与Aya Expanse 32B模型进行对比分析。

Result: 1. 英语到非洲语言的迁移效果因语言和层级而异；“非洲vs西方”的对比方向在非洲语言间比在非洲语言与控制语言间更一致；尼日利亚境内约鲁巴语和伊博语与豪萨语的关联度高于平均水平；2. 针对不同国家的引导方向估计归因率差异显著（0.63-0.81），多数输出通过结构连贯性测试；3. 与Aya Expanse 32B的对比显示结果依赖于具体的表征测量方法。

Conclusion: 研究证实了采样语言中存在区域和语系的表征模式，并证明了在英语输入下可实现针对特定非洲国家的文化引导，强调了表征测量方法对评估结果的重要性。

Abstract: We study how Gemma 4 31B represents African languages and responds to cultural steering. The first study compares nine African languages and three controls using probes, contrast directions, and measures of representation similarity. Transfer from English varies across languages and layers. Directions representing an Africa versus West contrast are more aligned among the African languages than between these languages and the controls at several layers. The comparison across language families passes the reported Holm threshold at five of twelve layers, although dependence between language pairs limits the statistical interpretation. Within Nigeria, Yoruba and Igbo are more aligned than the average of their pairs with Hausa at eleven of twelve layers. The second study uses separate English data to construct directions for Nigeria, Ghana, Kenya, and South Africa. Under union scoring at the selected strengths, estimated differences in attribution rates from random directions range from 0.63 to 0.81. Most outputs pass the automated structural coherence screen. Comparisons with Aya Expanse 32B show that results depend on the representation measure. Together, the studies document regional and family patterns in the sampled representations and country steering in English.

</details>


### [147] [The Canonical Order Problem: When Large Language Models Are Unreliable Knowledge Bases for Multi-Valued Relations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36209)
*Timo Pierre Schrader,Annemarie Friedrich,Simon Razniewski,Lukas Lange*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现大语言模型（LLM）在生成多值关系时存在“规范顺序问题”，即内部概率分布倾向于按特定顺序（如字母或时间）组织实体。机制分析表明生成过程分为候选检索、内部排序和选择三个阶段，违背此内部顺序的提示会导致生成完整集合的可靠性显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM常被用作知识库，但现有工作多关注提取单一关系三元组，而现实世界中的关系多为多值的，需要生成实体集合。本文旨在探究LLM如何表示和生成这些多值关系。

Method: 通过机制分析（mechanistic analysis），研究LLM在处理多值关系时的内部表示与生成过程，识别出“规范顺序问题”（canonical order problem），并将集合生成分解为三个阶段：候选实体检索、内部排序及下一个元素的选择。

Result: LLM内部将多值关系按照规范顺序（如字母顺序或时间顺序）进行组织。当提示要求构建的知识库偏离这种内部规范顺序时，LLM生成完整多值关系集合的可靠性会显著降低。

Conclusion: LLM在多值关系生成上受限于其内部的规范排序机制。为了获得可靠的完整集合输出，提示工程应考虑到或利用这一内部顺序特性，避免与之冲突。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as knowledge bases (KBs) due to the vast amount of knowledge they acquire during pre-training. While many works focus on extracting single relational triples, most real-world relations are multi-valued and require generating sets of entities.
  In this paper, we investigate how LLMs represent and generate multi-valued relations. We identify the canonical order problem: The probabilistic distributions inside LLMs organize many multi-valued relations according to a canonical ordering (e.g., alphabetical or chronological). Through mechanistic analysis, we show that set generation in LLMs can be thought of in terms of three phases: (1) retrieval of candidate entities, (2) internal sorting, and (3) selection of the next element. As a result, prompts aiming to construct KBs that deviate from this internal canonical ordering lead to a markedly reduced reliability of LLMs when aiming to generate complete sets for multi-valued relations.

</details>


### [148] [Lost in Translation: Measuring the Effect of Non-Native English on End User Performance of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36214)
*Yusheng Zhou,Eleanor Lin,David Jurgens*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究揭示大型语言模型（LLM）对非英语母语用户的响应质量存在系统性差距。通过构建FABLE数据集，发现模型虽不传播表面错误，但会镜像用户提示词中的高层修辞和词汇质量，导致非母语用户获得更低质量和流畅度的回答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管非英语母语者越来越多地使用LLM，但他们获得的响应质量系统性地低于流利使用者。由于语言流利度包含机械准确性、词汇使用、组织结构和话语连贯性等多个复合特征，目前尚不清楚哪些具体特征导致了这一差距。

Method: 引入FABLE数据集，该数据集包含从17.4万个真实写作任务用户提示词衍生出的190,911个受控英语提示变体。评估了34个开源LLM在这些提示上的响应表现。

Result: 发现明显的不对称性：模型不会将拼写错误等表面错误传播到输出中，但会镜像用户提示词中存在的高层修辞和词汇质量。最不流利与最流利提示之间的响应整体质量存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 研究突显了LLM在非英语母语用户群体中的关键性能差异，表现为提供的回答质量较低且流畅度较差，这源于模型对用户提示词高层语言特征的镜像效应。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used by people whose first language is not English, yet these users have been shown to receive systematically lower-quality responses than fluent speakers. Which specific features of non-native English drive this gap remains unclear, because fluency is itself a composite of mechanical accuracy, vocabulary use, organization, and discourse coherence. Here, we introduce FABLE, a controlled dataset of 190,911 English prompt variants derived from 174K real user prompts for writing-related tasks. Evaluating responses from 34 open-weight LLMs, we find a clear asymmetry; while models do not propagate surface errors such as misspellings into their outputs, models do mirror higher-level rhetorical and lexical qualities present in the user's prompt. Further, the overall quality of responses differs substantially between the least- and most-fluent prompts. These results highlight a key LLM performance disparity for non-native English LLM users, resulting in both lower-quality and less-fluent answers.

</details>


### [149] [CineSubBench: Evaluating LLMs on Long-Form Narrative and Cultural Understanding from Multilingual Movie Subtitles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36218)
*Mir Tafseer Nayeem,Susmoy Chakraborty,Davood Rafiei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了CineSubBench，这是一个用于评估大语言模型（LLM）长上下文电影理解能力的基准测试。该基准包含6种语言的1,012部电影字幕数据，涵盖7项任务，旨在解决当前LLM在电影叙事整合、多语言解释和文化判断方面评估不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM在法律、医疗等领域已有广泛评估，但在电影领域仍相对被忽视。电影理解需要整合长篇叙事、进行多语言解读以及基于文化背景的受众判断，这为LLM提供了独特的挑战。

Method: 构建了CineSubBench基准，包含1,012部电影的完整字幕覆盖（6种语言，共6,072条字幕轨，813万条带时间戳条目）。设计了匹配的多任务、多语言和多文化（MultiX）评估设置，包括7项任务：叙事重建与抽象、类型预测、适龄性判断、基于十个国家分级系统的评级预测，以及基于字幕的语言安全性检测。

Result: 在9个LLM上的实验显示：情节前提的恢复比完整事件摘要更可靠；跨语言一致性在不同模型和语言间差异显著；各国分级系统揭示了不同的校准模式；强粗俗用语比轻微不雅用语更容易 grounding。

Conclusion: CineSubBench确立了电影作为LLM长上下文评估的新领域，并提供了一个统一的基准，用于衡量模型在叙事、多语言、文化及证据 grounding 方面的能力。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly evaluated in specialized domains such as law, medicine, software engineering, and cybersecurity, yet film remains comparatively underexplored despite requiring long-form narrative integration, multilingual interpretation, and culturally situated audience judgments. We introduce CineSubBench, a benchmark for evaluating long-context film understanding from multilingual movie subtitles. A subtitle track represents a film as thousands of short, temporally ordered utterances from which models must reconstruct characters, relationships, events, causal progression, and themes without explicit scene or event structure. CineSubBench contains 1,012 films with complete subtitle coverage in six languages, yielding 6,072 tracks and 8.13M timestamped subtitle entries. It provides a matched multi-task, multilingual, and multicultural (MultiX) evaluation setting: seven tasks span narrative reconstruction and abstraction, genre prediction, age suitability, country-specific motion-picture ratings across ten national classification systems, and subtitle-grounded language safety. Across nine LLMs, plot premises are recovered more reliably than event-complete synopses; cross-lingual consistency varies substantially across models and languages; national rating systems expose distinct calibration patterns; and strong profanity is far easier to ground than mild obscenity. CineSubBench establishes film as a long-context LLM evaluation domain and provides a unified benchmark for measuring narrative, multilingual, cultural, and evidence-grounding capabilities.

</details>


### [150] [Cognitive Expert Language Models Better Align with the Corresponding Brain Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36239)
*Zhivar Sourati,Mengxuan Helen Wu,Nona Ghazizadeh,Jonas Kaplan,Morteza Dehghani,Samuel A. Nastase*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究证明，针对特定认知领域（如感知、空间、推理等）构建的专家大语言模型（LLM），比通用模型更能精准预测大脑中对应功能区域的活动。这表明“一刀切”的评估方法可能掩盖了模型在大脑不同区域表现上的特异性差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM与大脑对齐研究通常采用单一模型预测全脑活动并汇总性能，这种方法忽视了大脑各区域的功能特异性，可能导致对模型实际预测能力的误判。

Method: 通过提示（prompting）和微调（fine-tuning）技术，为六个认知领域（感知、空间、数值、推理、社交、抽象处理）构建专家LLM变体。随后，测试这些专家模型是否比非相关领域的专家或通用模型更准确地预测与之对应的特定脑区fMRI活动。实验涵盖三种基础模型和三个fMRI数据集，并进行了控制分析以排除表面级或非认知干预的影响。

Result: 1. 每个专家模型的表征与其对应的目标脑区对齐程度显著高于其他专家模型，且在提示和微调两种策略下均成立。
2. 这种对齐特异性在多种基础模型和数据集中具有一致性。
3. 控制分析显示，非认知层面的干预无法产生同等程度的对齐效果。
4. 模型专业化改变了区域特定的对齐模式，但并未显著改变整体预测准确率。

Conclusion: 基于认知领域专业化的模型能更好地捕捉大脑的功能特异性。简单地将模型性能跨脑区汇总可能会掩盖特定模型在特定区域的优势或劣势，因此评估LLM-大脑对齐时应考虑脑区的功能分工。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can predict human brain activity across a variety of brain regions during natural language comprehension. Typically, however, LLM-brain alignment is measured using one model for different regions of the brain, and then model performance is summarized across regions. This one-model-fits-all approach ignores the functional specialization of brain regions. In this study, we assess whether a model oriented toward a particular cognitive domain aligns better with the brain system dedicated to that domain. Through prompting and fine-tuning, we first build expert LLM variants for six domains: sensory, spatial, numerical, reasoning, social, and abstract processing. We then examine whether each expert best predicts activity in the brain region associated with the corresponding cognitive domain. Consistent with our hypotheses, each expert's representations align more closely with the brain system most associated with the matching domain than do other experts. This holds under both prompting and fine-tuning, across three base models and three fMRI datasets. In a series of control analyses, we show that this model-brain alignment is specific to cognitive domain interventions; non-cognitive and surface-level interventions do not result in comparable alignment. Specializing models shifts regional alignment while leaving aggregate prediction accuracy largely unchanged, suggesting that summarizing alignment across regions may obscure regional differences in performance for specific models.

</details>


### [151] [Learning from Teacher Continuations at Student States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36246)
*Haojin Wang,Dylan Zhang,Huaibo Chen,Suhao Yu,Yihang Sun,Zhanyang Jin,Jiaying Ye,Dianqi Li,Prasanna Sattigeri,Kamal Youcef-Toumi,Hao Peng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出OLIVE（在线干预）方法，通过学生生成前缀、教师自回归续写、学生基于交叉熵更新的方式，解决现有蒸馏方法的局限，在推理和代理任务上优于离线SFT和OPD，且训练效率更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有知识蒸馏方法的三大局限性：(1) 离线监督微调(SFT)中固定教师轨迹导致的序列协变量偏移；(2) 标记级在线策略蒸馏(OPD)中前缀失败引发的碎片化监督；(3) 分布匹配蒸馏中对教师标记概率的访问需求。

Method: OLIVE是一种在线蒸馏框架：迭代中由不断进化的学生策略生成新前缀，教师自回归续写，学生利用教师生成的标记计算交叉熵进行更新。采用异步实现以减少训练时间。

Result: OLIVE在相同GPU小时成本下推理性能高于OPD，异步实现使总训练时间减少23.8%。在硬推理任务和代理任务上持续优于现有蒸馏方法。仅使用GPT-5.4-mini文本，连续训练OLIVE在ScienceWorld上比离线SFT提升13%，且在离线蒸馏停滞后仍能改进，同时更好地保留学生的通用能力和可塑性。

Conclusion: OLIVE是一种有效且高效的在线语言模型蒸馏方法，适用于现代后训练场景，能克服传统方法的缺陷并提升性能与效率。

Abstract: We present OLIVE (OnLine InterVEntion). At each iteration, the evolving student policy generates a new prefix, the teacher continues it autoregressively, and the student is updated using cross-entropy computed on the teacher-generated tokens. Each design choice targets a corresponding limitation of existing distillation methods: (1) sequential covariate shift in offline supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on fixed teacher trajectories, (2) fragmented supervision under prefix failure in token-level on-policy distillation (OPD), and (3) the need for access to teacher token probabilities in distribution-matching distillation. OLIVE achieves higher reasoning performance than OPD (with a top-16 KL approximation) at comparable GPU-hour cost. Our asynchronous implementation further reduces OLIVE's total training time by 23.8\%. We evaluate OLIVE on both hard reasoning tasks and agentic tasks which reflects modern post-training scenarios, and it consistently outperforms existing distillation methods under the same training budget. By regenerating prefixes from the evolving student, OLIVE continues improving after offline distillation plateaus while better preserving the general capabilities and plasticity of the student. Using only text from GPT-5.4-mini, continuously training with OLIVE outperforms offline SFT from the same teacher by 13\% on ScienceWorld. These results support OLIVE as an effective and efficient approach to online language-model distillation.

</details>


### [152] [Population Fidelity: Evaluating Population Representativeness in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36253)
*Neemias B. da Silva,Martin Lukk,Ali Sutani,Abhishek Moturu,Harris Yang,Daniel Silver,Matt Ratto,Thiago H. Silva*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出“群体保真度”（Population Fidelity）评估框架，用于衡量大语言模型（LLM）对人类态度分布的模拟准确性。研究发现现有模型不仅压缩态度范围，还错误分配群体变异；文化微调虽提升总体对齐，但未改善群体内差异表征。该框架提供多维度评估及复用资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明LLM生成的响应会压缩人群中的态度范围，并可能以不同方式错误代表特定子群体。需要一种能够区分LLM响应代表人群所需关键条件的评估框架，以超越简单的聚合一致性指标。

Method: 引入“群体保真度”评估框架，包含三个维度：群体级准确性、组间变异量及其结构。通过两个实验验证：1）复现先前的“机器偏见”研究并应用新框架分析多个模型；2）评估“文化微调”这一改进模型代表性方法的效果。

Result: 1）不良表征不仅源于组间变异不足，还源于变异被错误地分配给不正确的群体；2）文化微调能改善与调查中心的对齐，但未能改善对群体内差异的表征，这是聚合一致性指标无法捕捉的区别。

Conclusion: 代表一个人群需要模型同时重现人类态度变异的多个特征。该框架组织这些特征，并提供可复用的代码、数据和训练好的模型，用于跨实质领域评估群体保真度和评估提出的对齐方法。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) show considerable potential in simulating human attitudes and preferences. Prior work finds that LLM-generated responses can compress the range of attitudes found within populations and misrepresent particular subgroups in ways that vary across models and topics. We introduce Population Fidelity, an evaluation framework that distinguishes key conditions required for a set of LLM-generated responses to represent a population. It incorporates three dimensions: group-level accuracy, the amount of between-group variation, and the structure of that variation. We demonstrate the framework's utility in two ways. First, we reproduce a prior study of "machine bias" in LLM survey responses and apply the framework to its models and more recent ones, showing that poor representation reflects not only insufficient between-group variation but also variation assigned to the wrong groups. Second, we evaluate one proposed approach to improving models' population representativeness: cultural fine-tuning. We find that cultural fine-tuning can improve alignment with the survey center without improving the representation of within-population differences, a distinction that measures of aggregate agreement do not capture. We argue that representing a population requires models to reproduce several features of human attitudinal variation simultaneously. Our framework organizes these features and provides reusable code, data, and trained models for evaluating population fidelity across substantive domains and assessing proposed alignment methods.

</details>


### [153] [HeurEvo: Agentic Evolution of Hybrid Solver-Augmented Heuristics for Time-Critical Mathematical Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36303)
*Feijie Wu,Hugo Barbalho,Konstantina Mellou,Marco Molinaro,Jing Gao,Ishai Menache,Xinzhi Zhang,Sirui Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出HeurEvo框架，通过智能体协同进化算法结构、代码实现和组件池，在严格时间约束下自动发现高质量启发式算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有启发式设计方法通常孤立地改进启发式组件或求解器配置，缺乏对计算资源分配、求解器利用及整体算法结构的整体适应性优化，难以满足实际应用中严格运行时间的限制。

Method: 构建HeurEvo计划-代码-组件共同进化框架：规划器决定组件组合与时间分配，编码器生成可执行代码，组件进化器更新共享组件池；基于岛屿进化框架，由解释器智能体提供执行反馈以指导计划与实现的共同进化。

Result: 在多种组合优化基准测试和MIPLIB实例上，HeurEvo能在有限时间内找到匹配或超越最先进求解器的高质量解；在六边形堆积等非线性几何问题上，取得了优于以往最佳报告的结果。

Conclusion: 联合搜索算法结构与实现对于智能启发式设计具有显著价值，能够有效提升复杂优化问题的求解效率与质量。

Abstract: Recent advances in agentic heuristic design use AI agents and execution feedback to automate algorithm discovery for challenging optimization problems. In many practical settings, high-quality solutions must be obtained under strict runtime constraints, motivating hybrid approaches that combine problem-specific heuristics with powerful mathematical programming solvers. However, existing approaches typically improve heuristic components within predefined procedures or tune solver configurations in isolation. This limits holistic adaptation of where to allocate computation, how to leverage solvers, and how to refine the overall algorithmic structure. To address these limitations, we propose HeurEvo, an automated plan--code--component co-evolution framework that jointly evolves the high-level algorithmic structures, their implementations, and a shared pool of reusable components. A planner determines which algorithmic components to use, how to combine them, and how to allocate runtime across stages, a coder realizes the resulting plan as executable code, while a component evolver updates the shared component pool. Within an island-based evolutionary framework, plans and implementations co-evolve with feedback from an interpreter agent that analyzes execution results and identifies opportunities for improvement. Across diverse combinatorial optimization benchmarks and challenging MIPLIB instances, HeurEvo finds high-quality solutions within tight runtime budgets, often matching or surpassing state-of-the-art optimization solvers given hours or days of computation. On several nonlinear geometry problems such as hexagon packing, it also improves upon the best previously reported results. These results highlight the value of jointly searching over algorithmic structure and implementation for agentic heuristic design.

</details>


### [154] [Training LLMs to Verbalize Evaluation Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36316)
*Usman Anwar,Sahar Abdelnabi,David Krueger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出言语化训练（VT）方法，通过强化学习让大语言模型更准确地表达评估意识（EA），同时保持潜在行为和信念稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决大语言模型在审计与部署中因评估意识（EA）导致行为差异的问题，且难以测量和量化这种意识。

Method: 利用模型自发的言语化作为存在意识的证据，在言语化前截断轨迹生成训练前缀，并使用强化学习目标以校准方式增加言语化输出，而非直接监督潜在信念。

Result: 在Qwen3.6-35B-A3B、Kimi K2.6和Inkling模型上，VT使言语化的评估意识提升2.4-2.9倍，并迁移到未见过的智能体设置中；测量的潜在意识和行为保持稳定；因果实验表明言语化反映了模型获取的更丰富知识。

Conclusion: VT能有效增强大模型对评估意识的言语表达能力，为理解和控制模型在审计场景下的行为提供了新途径。

Abstract: Evaluation awareness (EA) can cause large language models (LLMs) to behave differently during audits than in deployment, yet measuring and accounting for EA remains challenging. We introduce verbalization training (VT), a method for making LLMs less reticent about verbalizing evaluation awareness while avoiding to supervise the latent belief itself. VT uses a model's spontaneous verbalizations as evidence that awareness is present and truncates each rollout immediately before the verbalization, producing training prefixes at which the model is presumed to be aware. The model is then trained with an RL objective designed to increase verbalization in a calibrated way. Across Qwen3.6-35B-A3B, Kimi K2.6, and Inkling, VT increases verbalized EA by 2.4-2.9 times and transfers to held-out agentic settings, while measured latent EA and behavior remain largely stable. In a causal experiment, we independently implant meta-knowledge about evaluations through synthetic-document fine-tuning and show that VT-induced verbalizations reflect the richer knowledge acquired by the model.

</details>


### [155] [DeepRewind: Predicting and Repairing Premature Commitments in Deep Research Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36344)
*Amirhossein Abaskohi,Amirhossein Dabiriaghdam,Lele Wang,Peter West,Giuseppe Carenini*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DeepRewind 是一种用于深度研究代理的可逆控制层，通过预测和监控机制减少过早承诺，显著提升了洞察召回率并降低了错误推理风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度研究代理在长期迭代调查中容易在证据不足时过早确立观点，导致后续推理强化错误解释，缺乏对认知状态的可逆性管理。

Method: 引入 DeepRewind 作为加法控制层，将代理的认知状态表示为包含来源、证据、主张等的类型化图；利用基于提示的世界模型预测中间结论的影响及可逆性（基于假设收窄、信息丢失等指标）；通过二进制控制器阻止高风险承诺，并通过一致性监控器执行依赖感知的回滚。

Result: 在 DRBench 和 LiveDRBench 基准测试中，相比 Open Deep Research，DeepRewind 将洞察召回率提高了 3.6 个百分点，并将过早承诺减少了 59.1%。

Conclusion: 通过引入可逆性和前瞻性评估机制，DeepRewind 有效改善了深度研究代理的推理可靠性，防止了因证据不足导致的错误固化。

Abstract: Deep-research agents conduct long-horizon investigations through iterative search, evidence evaluation, belief revision, and synthesis. However, they may commit to claims before sufficient evidence is available, causing later reasoning to reinforce an incorrect interpretation. We introduce DeepRewind, an additive control layer for reversible deep research that represents the agent's evolving epistemic state as a typed graph of sources, evidence, claims, hypotheses, assumptions, commitments, plans, and drafts. Before accepting an intermediate conclusion, a prompt-based world model predicts its impact and estimates reversibility based on hypothesis narrowing, information loss, recovery cost, and contradiction-trigger coverage. A binary controller blocks risky commitments, while a consistency monitor performs dependency-aware rollback when later evidence invalidates them. Across DRBench and LiveDRBench, DeepRewind improves insight recall by 3.6 percentage points and reduces premature commitments by 59.1% relative to Open Deep Research.

</details>


### [156] [Calibrated to Whom? Persona and Language Effects on Cultural Values in JEV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36399)
*Bushra Asseri,Abdulaziz Asseri*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究审计了决策型语言模型JEV的文化价值观，发现其回答具有高度可重复性；当设定为沙特或美国角色时，能部分复现人类文化差异（英语中87%，阿拉伯语中62%），且语言本身而非角色描述影响了跨文化差异的幅度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策型语言模型因返回概率而非生成文本，适合作为调查受访者或评估者，但其内在文化价值观尚不明确，需进行系统性审计以了解其是否及如何反映不同文化背景的特征。

Method: 使用Values Survey Module 2013中的24个项目，对JEV模型进行审计。设置12组匹配的沙特和美国角色以及无角色情境，在英语和阿拉伯语两种语言下，通过8种不同的请求 formulation 方式，共收集28.8万个答案，并分析ICC（组内相关系数）、文化维度偏移、语言和角色对结果的影响。

Result: 1. JEV的回答高度可重复（ICC 0.997）；无角色时答案接近其美国角色设定。
2. 设定沙特角色时，答案向人类沙特-美国差异方向移动，复现了87%（英语）和62%（阿拉伯语）的差异幅度，但长期导向维度相反。
3. 语言交叉分析表明，阿拉伯语中差异较小是由题目语言导致，而非角色描述语言。
4. 年龄对画像的影响程度与国籍相当，性别对沙特角色的影响大于美国角色，且JEV在阿拉伯语和沙特角色下置信度较低。
5. 所有请求设计均呈现一致模式，尽管模型不生成文本。

Conclusion: 决策型语言模型能够模拟特定文化角色的价值观倾向，但其表现受输入语言显著影响，且在非母语或特定文化角色下置信度降低，提示在使用此类模型进行跨文化评估时需考虑语言和角色设定的交互效应。

Abstract: Decision-only language models return a probability for every answer option instead of generating text, which makes them attractive as survey respondents and as judges. We audit the cultural values of one such model, TypeSafe's JEV, with the Values Survey Module 2013. We asked it the 24 items as 12 matched Saudi and 12 matched American personas and without a persona, in English and Arabic, under eight ways of formulating the request (288,000 answers). JEV's answers were highly repeatable (ICC 0.997), and without a persona they resembled those of its own American personas. When the persona was Saudi rather than American, the answers moved in the direction of the human Saudi-US difference, reproducing 87% of its size in English but 62% in Arabic, with long-term orientation reversed. A language cross shows that the smaller difference in Arabic comes from the language of the items, not from the language of the persona description. Age shifted the profiles about as much as nationality, gender shifted them more for Saudi than for American personas, and JEV was less confident in Arabic and for Saudi personas. These patterns held in every request design, although the model never generates text.

</details>


### [157] [Eternal Sunshine of the Spotless Mind: Systematically Erasing LLM's Memories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36414)
*Olga Ohrimenko*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对持久化LLM无法真正遗忘用户请求删除的信息的问题，提出DeLLM框架。该框架通过构建消息依赖的溯源图（provenance graph）来动态确定需删除的内容，解决了因对话上下文依赖导致信息残留的问题，实现了高删除率并保持模型效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久化LLM利用外部存储积累用户交互记忆以突破上下文窗口限制，具有广泛实用价值。然而，当前LLM在用户请求下无法真正删除已共享的信息，即使声称已遗忘或在有限上下文中运行亦如此。研究旨在解决这一隐私与数据控制难题，探索“LLM记忆删除”的新方向。

Method: 提出DeLLM框架。该方法不简单地移除匹配删除请求的消息，而是通过动态构建相关上下文和维持消息溯源图（provenance graph），识别并处理由自然对话引入的消息依赖性，从而准确确定必须删除的消息集合。

Result: 实验表明，DeLLM能够实现较高的信息删除率，同时在保持模型功能效用（utility）方面表现良好，有效解决了朴素删除方法因忽略消息依赖而导致信息残留的问题。

Conclusion: 简单移除消息不足以实现LLM记忆的彻底删除，因为存在消息间的依赖关系。DeLLM通过维护溯源图和动态上下文管理，为LLM提供了可靠的信息删除机制，平衡了隐私保护与模型效用。

Abstract: We consider persistent LLMs that accumulate memories of their interactions with a user over time. Such LLMs maintain memories using external storage, which they can query to overcome the limitations of a fixed context window. Such systems have numerous practical applications, as they can draw on all past interactions when responding to user queries.
  In this paper, we ask whether LLMs can forget information shared with them upon a user's request. We find that current LLMs fail to delete such information---even when they claim to have forgotten it and even when operating with a limited context. To this end, we consider a new direction of study: Deletion of LLM Memories.
  We show that naively removing messages that match a user's deletion request is insufficient, since conversations naturally introduce message dependencies that cause information to persist. To correctly handle deletion requests, we propose the DeLLM framework. It dynamically constructs relevant context for each LLM query and maintains a provenance graph of messages to determine which ones must be removed during deletion. Our experiments show that DeLLM achieves a high deletion rate while maintaining utility.

</details>


### [158] [MemFold: Learning Compact Soft Memory for Long-Context Personalization via On-Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36435)
*Jingxuan Wu,Yuzhe Yang,Yiqiao Huang,Chengzhi Liu,Qingni Wang,Chengxuan Qian,Shutong Wu,Jiawei Zhang,Xin Eric Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MemFold通过行为优化固定预算的软记忆，将文本记忆压缩为连续向量，利用奖励和蒸馏信号训练，在长历史对话场景中显著提升准确率并具备跨领域迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在长期用户交互中难以平衡信息保留与使用：保留文本导致输入增长，压缩为潜在向量则通常仅针对重建或模仿进行训练，而非针对实际行为优化，且评分标准往往基于用户未生成的序列。

Method: 提出MemFold框架，将查询条件的文本记忆压缩为K个连续向量作为读者（reader）的记忆接口。训练过程包含两个互补信号：一是针对任务结果的组相对奖励（group-relative rewards），二是置信度门控的策略内蒸馏（confidence-gated on-policy distillation），其中冻结的文本记忆教师模型对学生采样的token进行重评分，且仅在推理时移除教师模型。

Result: 在三个Qwen骨干网络上，MemFold在PersonaMem-32K和PersonaMem-128K数据集上取得了最高的准确率，且在更长历史记录下优势更明显。该方法无需目标域训练即可迁移至PrefEval和LongMemEval。消融实验表明，任务增益主要来自奖励项，教师信号带来额外增益；记忆干预显示读者依赖软记忆中的实例特定内容。

Conclusion: MemFold通过直接优化记忆支持的行为而非单纯的重建或模仿，有效解决了长上下文记忆中的信息利用问题，证明了基于行为的软记忆优化在长历史对话理解中的优越性和泛化能力。

Abstract: An assistant that serves the same user over a long horizon has to answer from what that user has revealed: which preferences still hold, which were revised, and which constraints apply now. Retaining that information is not the same as acting on it, and the two are usually optimized as if they were. Keeping the information as text makes the reader's input grow with the retained history, while compressing it into a fixed number of latent vectors bounds the interface but is typically trained to reconstruct text or imitate reference answers, both of which are scored on sequences the reader never produced. We present MemFold, which optimizes a fixed-budget soft memory by the behavior it supports. A query-conditioned textual memory is compressed into K continuous vectors that form the reader's memory interface, and the reader is then trained on its own rollouts under two complementary signals: group-relative rewards for task outcomes, and confidence-gated on-policy distillation in which a frozen textual-memory teacher re-scores the student's sampled tokens under the textual memory. The teacher is never sampled from, so supervision stays on the student's current distribution and adds no autoregressive decoding; at inference it is removed entirely. Across three Qwen backbones, MemFold attains the highest accuracy we measure on PersonaMem-32K and PersonaMem-128K, with margins that widen at the longer history length, and transfers to PrefEval and LongMemEval without target-domain training. Ablations attribute most of the task gain to the reward term and a smaller additional gain to the teacher signal, and memory interventions show that the reader depends on the instance-specific content of its soft memory.

</details>


### [159] [Reliable Parallel Decoding in Masked Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36452)
*Zhenghao He,Bohan Liu,Guangzhi Xiong,Aidong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出无训练的可靠并行解码（RPD）方法，通过层预测稳定性和累积熵预算解决掩码扩散语言模型中并行提交不可靠的问题，在数学推理和代码生成任务中实现最高吞吐量和准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码扩散语言模型（MDLMs）虽能并行预测多个掩码Token以提升效率，但同一前向传播中的预测在共同提交时可靠性不足，需研究何时并行提交是可靠的。

Method: 提出可靠并行解码（RPD），一种无需训练的方法：基于层间预测稳定性选择候选者，结合最终置信度，并在前置掩码位置的累积熵预算下进行提交；推迟上游上下文不确定的预测，其余候选者并行提交，不依赖固定块调度。

Result: 在LLaDA和Dream上的数学推理及代码生成基准测试中，RPD实现了所评估方法中最高的解码吞吐量，同时保持或提升了准确率。

Conclusion: 诊断显示仅凭置信度无法确定可靠提交顺序，而层稳定性是关键；RPD通过动态策略有效平衡了并行效率与预测可靠性。

Abstract: Masked diffusion language models (MDLMs) can generate text efficiently by predicting multiple masked tokens in parallel, but predictions from the same forward pass are not necessarily reliable when committed together. We study when parallel commitment is reliable. Our diagnostics show that confidence alone does not determine a reliable commitment order: confident predictions near the end of the sequence can fix an answer before its supporting computations are established, and downstream predictions become less reliable as the uncertainty of their upstream context grows. At the same time, a single forward pass can already resolve several masked tokens, and predictions that remain stable across the final layers are more likely to be correct. Based on these findings, we propose Reliable Parallel Decoding (RPD), a training-free method that selects candidates by layerwise prediction stability and final confidence, and commits them under a cumulative entropy budget over their preceding masked positions. RPD defers predictions with uncertain upstream context while committing the remaining candidates in parallel, without relying on a fixed block schedule. Across mathematical reasoning and code generation benchmarks on LLaDA and Dream, RPD achieves the highest decoding throughput among the evaluated methods while maintaining or improving accuracy.

</details>


### [160] [Memory Consolidation Flattens the Temporal Shape of User Facts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36457)
*Sugam Panthi,Muhaiminul Yeamin,Siyan Luo,Rabab Abdelfattah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 长期记忆系统存在“体貌扁平化”问题，即丢失动作是否持续进行的线索，导致后续模型难以判断事实的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决长期记忆系统在存储用户信息时，因忽略时间形式（如进行时与一般现在时）而丢失关键语境线索的问题，防止后续模型基于过时信息做出错误决策。

Method: 提出“体貌扁平化”概念及LAPSE基准测试；评估多种记忆写入模型（mem0, Graphiti, Letta等）在处理不同时间形式语句时的表现，并分析读取模型对扁平化信息的反应。

Result: 1. 记忆写入模型选择性地进行体貌扁平化，倾向于将进行时简化为一般现在时（244/381对中仅单向发生）。2. 这种不对称性在所有测试的模型配置和管道中均存在。3. 仅改变动词形式即可显著影响读者模型对事实有效性的判断；在可询问用户的场景下，扁平化笔记导致更多未验证行动。

Conclusion: 记忆写入过程会移除后续模型用于判断行为时机的重要证据（即事实是否仍然成立），这一缺陷在现有主流记忆系统中普遍存在且未被常规任务检测到。

Abstract: Long-term memory systems turn conversations into short stored notes. A note can keep a user fact while losing evidence about whether the fact still holds. For example, "I am driving a Peugeot" can become "The user drives a Peugeot," which drops the cue that the activity is ongoing. We call this aspectual flattening and measure it with LAPSE, a benchmark of matched user statements that differ only in temporal form. We find that memory writers flatten aspect selectively. Three writer models flattened the progressive statement but kept its simple-present match in 244 of 381 pairs, never the reverse. The asymmetry holds in all 11 model configurations tested and in the installed pipelines mem0, Graphiti, and Letta. The lost cue matters to later readers. In exploratory tests, changing only the stored verb shifted all three readers' estimates that a fact still holds. When readers could ask the user before acting, two of three acted without asking more often on flattened notes. Our planned memory-use task could not detect this, because readers there acted on almost every stored fact, even expired ones. Memory writing can thus remove evidence that later models use to decide whether to act.

</details>


### [161] [FinRT: Distilling Adaptive Red-Teaming Strategies into Reusable Adversarial Generators in Consumer Finance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36474)
*Rikhiya Ghosh,Himanshu Kumar,Sriram Venkatapathy,Sahil Wadhwa,Alexandre G. R. Day,Pranab Mohanty*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出FinRT框架，通过构建可复用的对抗性提示生成器，在消费金融领域的大语言模型红队测试中显著提升攻击成功率（32.9% vs 17.2%）和最大对抗严重程度（提升33%），同时保持语义多样性并实现跨模型迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化红队测试方法在攻击有效性与生成成本之间权衡不足，且未将覆盖度、严重性和多样性作为联合目标进行优化；受监管行业（如消费金融）的用户查询可能利用大语言模型漏洞导致安全失败。

Method: 引入FinRT结构化框架，从自适应红队测试策略中构建可复用的对抗性提示生成器，将面向目标的攻击生成为可复用组件，而非单次迭代搜索。

Result: 在六个消费者金融受害者模型上，FinRT显著优于自适应搜索基线：攻击成功率翻倍（32.9%对比17.2%），最大对抗严重程度提高33%，保留与迭代搜索方法相当的域内语义多样性，并表现出高跨模型迁移性及针对特定受害者家族的 specialization 模式。

Conclusion: FinRT通过可复用生成器机制，在降低重复计算成本的同时，实现了更高效的攻击覆盖、更高的严重性和良好的跨模型迁移能力，为受监管行业的大模型安全评估提供了更有效的方法。

Abstract: In regulated industries like consumer finance, seemingly harmless user queries can exploit large language model vulnerabilities, triggering safety failures and pushing responses dangerously close to policy limits. Existing automated red-teaming methods trade off attack effectiveness against generation cost, while treating coverage, severity, and diversity as incidental rather than joint objectives. We introduce FinRT, a structured framework that builds reusable adversarial prompt generators from adaptive red-teaming strategies. Across the six victim models in consumer finance, FinRT substantially outperforms adaptive search baselines while amortizing target-facing attack generation into a reusable generator. FinRT nearly doubles the attack success rate over the adaptive baseline Rainbow Teaming (32.9% vs. 17.2%), increases maximum adversarial severity by 33%, and preserves comparable intra-policy-domain semantic diversity to iterative search methods. Our method achieves high cross-model transferability while exhibiting distinct victim-family specialization patterns.

</details>


### [162] [Similar Choices, Different Attention: Cross-Modal Associations in Humans and Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36475)
*Sumin Hong,Katsumi Ibaraki,Renee Shi,David Chiang,Toby Jia-Jun Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究对比了视觉语言模型（VLMs）与人类在跨模态关联任务中的选择一致性和注意力分布。发现虽然微调后的VLMs能达到接近人类的决策准确率，但其注意力机制与人类眼动数据的匹配度甚至低于简单的中心偏差基线。结论指出，仅匹配人类的选择或注视模式不足以证明模型具备真正的人类对齐的跨模态处理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常因使用不同的刺激物或任务而难以直接比较人类与VLMs在跨模态关联（如“bouba/kiki”效应）上的表现。本研究旨在通过统一刺激物和任务，深入探究VLMs不仅在决策结果上，更在视觉注意力分配上是否与人类保持一致。

Method: 招募53名人类参与者，并与多个VLMs进行对比实验。向两者呈现相同的伪词和图像对，记录参与者的选择结果及眼动数据（已公开数据集）。通过计算选择一致性和注意力-眼动相关性来评估模型性能，并尝试通过微调模型选择或训练模型注意力来优化对齐效果。

Result: 1. 部分大型VLMs在任务选择上与人类高度一致；2. 然而，这些模型的注意力热力图与人类眼动数据的匹配度较低，甚至不如固定的高斯中心偏差基线；3. 在小规模VLMs上进行人类选择微调后，其选择一致性达到人类多数投票水平，但注意力匹配度仍不及基线；4. 专门训练模型以匹配人类眼动可提高注意力-眼动相关性，但并未提升选择一致性。

Conclusion: 单纯匹配人类的行为选择或注视模式，并不能作为模型具备人类对齐的跨模态处理能力的充分证据。模型可能在决策层面模仿人类，但在内在的视觉注意力机制上存在本质差异。

Abstract: Cross-modal associations are systematic pairings of features across modalities, such as the association of 'bouba' with round shapes and 'kiki' with sharp shapes. Prior work has compared humans and vision-language models (VLMs) on such associations, but often using different stimuli or tasks between humans and models. Here, we ask whether VLMs align with humans not only in choices, but also in where they look when making those choices. We study both VLMs and humans (N = 53), presenting them with the same stimuli, a pseudo-word and two images, and record participants' choices and eye movements, which we release. We find choice alignment in a few larger VLMs, but their saliency matches human gaze less closely than a center-bias baseline, a fixed Gaussian at the center of each image. Fine-tuning small VLMs on human choices brings their choice alignment to the level of a human majority-vote reference on unseen words and images, yet their attention still matches human gaze less closely than this baseline. Training model attention on human gaze raises attention-gaze correlation without improving choice alignment, and a single average gaze map per image position raises it by a similar amount. Matching human choices, or even human gaze patterns, is therefore not sufficient evidence of human-aligned cross-modal processing.

</details>


### [163] [Large-scale factor analysis shows machine intelligence is only partially interpretable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36515)
*Faiz Ghifari Haznitrama,Afrizal Hasbi Azizy,Faeyza Rishad Ardi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过因子分析大规模语言模型评估数据，发现并不存在一个统一的、可被标准基准代理的“通用智力”因素，挑战了当前以单一通用能力为目标的发展策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验语言模型开发中普遍存在的假设，即认知能力围绕着一个类似人类流体智力的通用、非领域特异性因素组织，且此前缺乏直接的大规模测试。

Method: 采用潜在变量方法，对涵盖1,618个语言模型和456个基准的13,251个评估分数进行因子分析，利用多种数据增密和插补方法处理稀疏数据集。

Result: 1. 通用智力因素（g factor）最多仅解释70.8%的性能方差，且在多数方案中占比更低；2.内容相似的基准不一定聚类；3. g因素不受任何共同主题主导，且缺乏证据表明其能被标准“智力”基准良好代理。

Conclusion: 反对将通用智力定义为语言模型发展中可操作的具体构造，指出针对单一概念能力的策略缺乏支持，因为其一阶能力通常是特异的且难以在实践中识别。

Abstract: A common assumption in language model development is that cognitive abilities are organized around a general, domain-free intelligence factor, like fluid intelligence in humans. This assumption is rarely tested directly, and prior attempts have done so only at a much smaller scale. We take a latent variable approach to intelligence in language models, similar to how psychometricians study psychological constructs. Performance in every specific problem set is influenced by a domain-specific and a domain-agnostic latent factor. Using factor analysis as a dimension-reduction technique, we analyzed 13,251 published evaluation scores covering 1,618 language models across 456 different text-only benchmarks. Due to the super-sparse nature of the dataset, we triangulate our analysis across different data densifiers and imputation methods. A robust pattern across different modes of bias is that 1. A general intelligence factor accounts for 70.8% of variance in model performance at our most generous estimate, and far less than that in most of our solutions, 2. Content-similar benchmarks do not necessarily cluster together, and 3. The $g$ factor is not dominated by any common theme, and there is a lack of evidence that it is well-proxied by standard "intelligence" benchmarks. Our findings go against current endeavors of defining, identifying, and targeting general intelligence as a tangible construct in language model development. This leaves the strategy of targeting a single conceptual ability without support, since the first-order abilities it would have to reach are often partially idiosyncratic and not identifiable in practice.

</details>


### [164] [When Updating Stops Being Learning: Rethinking LLM Self-Evolution via learnable information gain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36535)
*Chenxu Wang,Chaozhuo Li,Xinze Shi,Songyang Liu,Kyrie You Wu,Ziluowen Luo,Shun Zhang,Chenxi Li,Litian Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对大语言模型自进化中的性能退化问题，提出基于可学习信息增益的全局框架ATRI，通过衡量数据分布变化动态调整训练权重并适时停止，有效缓解性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决大语言模型在利用自身生成数据进行迭代改进时出现的“自进化退化”现象（即性能提升后停滞随后下降），指出现有方法仅关注组件层面而忽视了自进化系统的紧密耦合性。

Method: 提出基于可学习信息增益的整体框架，理论上该增益等于两轮数据分布的KL散度与熵变之和；实践中通过拟合小规模语言模型并利用负对数似然对新数据进行评分。据此提出ATRI（自适应训练调节机制），用于重加权样本并在信息增益持续偏低时跨轮次终止训练。

Result: 在主流数据集上的实验表明，该方法优于现有方案，能够有效抑制自进化过程中的性能衰退。

Conclusion: 将自进化视为紧密耦合系统并引入信息增益作为诊断指标，是实现稳定、高效大模型自我优化的有效途径。

Abstract: Self-evolution lets large language models (LLMs) improve iteratively using their own generated data, but often suffers from self-evolution degeneration: performance improves, plateaus, then declines. Existing methods address this issue at the component level, targeting either the Questioner or the Solver, and overlook that self-evolution is a tightly coupled system. We propose a holistic framework based on learnable information gain, which measures how much novel, parameterizable information a round provides relative to the previous round. Theoretically, this gain equals the Kullback-Leibler divergence between the two rounds' data distributions plus their entropy change. Practically, it is estimated by fitting a small language model to the previous round and scoring new data via negative log-likelihood. Based on this diagnostic, we propose ATRI (Adaptive Training Regulation via Information-gain), which reweights samples within a round and halts training across rounds when information gain remains low. Experiments on popular datasets demonstrate the superiority of our proposal.

</details>


### [165] [DraftTrace: A Multi-View Analytics Environment for AI-Integrated Writing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36544)
*Divyansh Chandarana,Sandipan De,Vivek Gupta*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出DraftTrace，一种能同时捕捉最终产品、写作过程和AI交互三种视图的写作环境，用于分析学生使用生成式AI辅助写作时的行为差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI改变了学生撰写作业的方式，仅凭最终成品已无法充分理解其产生过程，需要新的工具来解析这一过程。

Method: 开发DraftTrace系统，在81名研究生的NLP课程中部署，对比LLM自动生成、复制粘贴及正常输入的数据，并通过多视图分析（提交物、纵向、班级级）和初步讲师调查进行评估。

Result: 产品指标可区分文本 formulations 的差异，过程指标可区分文本输入方式的差异；结合两者有助于识别复制粘贴等特定情况；交互数据显示学生在写作不同阶段对AI助手的使用策略不同（早期澄清问题，后期验证答案）。

Conclusion: 多视图写作分析及其可解释性对于理解生成式AI辅助下的写作过程具有重要价值。

Abstract: Generative AI has changed how students produce writing assignments. The final artifact is no longer sufficient to understand the process through which it was produced. We introduce DraftTrace, a writing environment that jointly captures three complementary views of writing: the final product, the writing process and interactions with an integrated AI-assistant. DraftTrace reconstructs how a document develops over time and organizes these signals into submission, longitudinal, and class-level analytics for instructors. We deployed DraftTrace in a graduate NLP course with 81 students and compared their sessions with LLM-generated responses entered by automated tools and with copy-typed responses. While product measures distinguish differences in text formulation, process measures distinguish differences in how text is entered. Considering both views together helps characterize cases such as copy-typing. Interaction traces show that students use the assistant differently across stages of writing: to clarify the question at an early stage and to verify answers at a later stage. A preliminary instructor survey highlights the importance of multi-view writing analytics and their interpretability.

</details>


### [166] [Grounded Revision vs. Prior Injection: Probing Retrieval-Augmented Patent Claim Amendment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36550)
*Josepha Michiko Leo,Hyun-seok Min,Yehoon Jang,Irvan Zidny,Jin-Woo Chung,Sungchul Choi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究利用专利权利要求修改数据，通过大规模预注册实验和确定性评估指标，发现检索增强生成（RAG）在专利撰写中并未表现出明显的“先验注入”行为，检索效果微弱且不一致，模型更倾向于基于局部语境而非模板化引用进行修改。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管检索增强生成广泛用于专业写作，但现有研究很少测试检索是真正支持内容修订还是仅仅注入固定模板。专利审查场景提供了明确的“正确”标准（审查员指出的限制和引证文献），适合验证这一假设。

Method: 1. 发布包含7,385个USPTO案件的数据集（含XML对齐的前后权利要求、拒绝理由及引证文献）。2. 使用七项探针电池比较随机检索与结构匹配检索两种策略。3. 开发无需LLM评估的五通道确定性指标（C1-C3, C5为主，C4为补充）。4. 在四种前沿大语言模型（Claude Sonnet 4, Claude Haiku 4.5, GPT-5.4, GPT-4o-mini）上进行9,600次预注册调用，测试不同检索深度（k=1,3,5,10）及密集语义检索器的影响。

Result: 没有任何测试模型显示出可检测的经典“先验注入”行为；检索对结果的影响很小，且在随机检索与结构检索之间方向不一致。即使在密集语义检索器下，或改变检索深度时，零假设（无显著效应）保持不变。此外，修订的局部性揭示了模型间的差异，而模板通道未能捕捉到这一点。探索性的四格分类法中，“先验注入者”单元格为空。

Conclusion: 在专利权利要求修改任务中，检索增强生成并未如预期那样导致简单的模板化先验注入。模型表现出更复杂的、依赖局部语境的修订行为，现有的模板化评估方法可能忽略了这种细微差别。研究强调了在专业写作场景中重新评估RAG机制必要性。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation is widely used in professional writing, yet whether retrieval grounds revision or merely injects templates is rarely tested where "correct" has a definable meaning. Patent claim amendment supplies that signal: the examiner names the attacked limitation and cites prior art, providing per-case ground truth. We release three artifacts: (i) a corpus of 7,385 USPTO prosecution cases with XML-aligned pre/post claims, rejection, and cited prior art; (ii) a seven-probe battery comparing random and structural-match retrieval as two policies under a fixed prompt scaffold; (iii) a deterministic five-channel metric (C1-C3 and C5 in main, C4 supplementary) requiring no LLM evaluation. Across 9,600 pre-registered calls on four frontier LLMs (Claude Sonnet 4, Claude Haiku 4.5, GPT-5.4, GPT-4o-mini), no tested model exhibits detectable classical prior-injection behavior; retrieval effects are small and direction-inconsistent between random and structural retrieval, and the null is unchanged under a dense (semantic) retriever, across retrieval depths k in {1,3,5,10}, and under a paraphrase-sensitive grounding metric. Revision locality reveals a model-specific difference that the template channel misses. The four-cell taxonomy, which we treat as exploratory, leaves the prior-injector cell unoccupied.

</details>


### [167] [SEED: Self-Speculative Decoding via Implicit Encoder-Decoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36590)
*Hankun Lin,Patrick Pynadath,Ruqi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SEED（自推测编码器-解码器），通过复用验证阶段计算的深层上下文表示，以低成本生成高质量草稿，实现LLM推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有自推测解码方法中草稿质量与成本之间的权衡问题：早期退出法成本低但质量差，多token预测法质量高但计算成本高。

Method: 将标准仅解码器Transformer重新解释为隐式编码器-解码器结构；合并编码与验证步骤，缓存已验证前缀的深层上下文表示；在验证间隔期间，轻量级解码器基于缓存表示和先前草稿自回归地快速生成多个草稿token。

Result: 在4B规模模型上平均加速最高达2.7倍，优于早期退出和多token预测基线，比SOTA的EAGLE-3快28%，同时保持或提升生成质量。

Conclusion: SEED通过高效复用深层表示，实现了高性能、低成本的自推测解码，显著提升了大语言模型推理效率。

Abstract: Self-speculative decoding accelerates large language model (LLM) inference by drafting tokens from the target model itself, but faces a sharp tradeoff between the quality and cost of the draft. Early-exit methods produce drafts cheaply by terminating computation at intermediate layers, but forgo the deeper representations that later layers provide and thus suffer in draft quality. Multi-token prediction preserves draft quality by emitting from the model's final hidden states, but pays for a full forward pass to produce those states at every drafting step. We propose self-speculative encoder-decoder (SEED), a self-speculative method that obtains high-quality drafts cheaply by reusing the deep contextual representations already computed during verification. We reinterpret the standard decoder-only transformer as an implicit encoder-decoder: the first layers (encoder) build deep contextual representations, and the last few layers (decoder) emit tokens from them. Encoding and verification are merged into a single step: verification is performed by the full encoder-decoder, and the contextual representations of the verified prefix are cached for reuse during drafting. Drafting is therefore very fast: between verifications, the lightweight decoder drafts multiple tokens autoregressively, each conditioned on the cached representations and on preceding drafts. Experiments across multiple benchmarks show that SEED achieves up to 2.7$\times$ average speedup on 4B-scale models, outperforming both early-exit and MTP-style self-speculative baselines and running 28% faster than the state-of-the-art EAGLE-3, while preserving or even improving the generation quality of standard autoregressive fine-tuning. Code is available at https://github.com/lhk2004/SEED.

</details>


### [168] [Generating Edit-Inducing Questions for AI Research Manuscripts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36617)
*Sebastian Joseph,Zichao Wang,Jennifer Healey,Alexa Siu,Junyi Jessy Li,Ani Nenkova*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究大语言模型（LLM）生成能诱导论文修改问题的潜力。基于ICLR和NeurIPS的配对数据集，发现GPT生成的问题比人类审稿人覆盖更广、引发的修改更多，但“有效”比例较低；同时指出处理长上下文可能损害模型的推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估LLM在学术出版流程中的辅助价值，具体探究其能否通过生成高质量问题来促进论文草稿的改进，并与人类审稿人的表现进行对比。

Method: 使用ICLR和NeurIPS会议中提交的初稿与最终版（camera-ready）论文的配对数据作为数据集。比较带有完整论文上下文的GPT模型生成的问题与人类审稿人提出的问题，分析两者在诱导编辑（edit-inducing）方面的有效性及覆盖范围。

Result: 1. GPT生成的问题数量更多，且这些问题关联的编辑幅度更大，覆盖的内容范围比人类审稿人的问题更广。
2. 尽管绝对数量多，但GPT生成问题中实际具有“诱导编辑”作用的百分比远低于人类审稿人。
3. 分析证实自动化提问对作者有益，但也发现让模型关注长上下文会降低其产生有用输出的推理能力。

Conclusion: LLM生成的自动化问题在帮助作者完善论文方面具有潜在益处，特别是在提供广泛反馈方面优于人类。然而，存在信噪比低的问题，且长上下文处理对模型推理有负面影响，这为未来优化AI辅助写作工具提供了方向。

Abstract: We study the ability of LLMs to generate edit-inducing questions whose answer will improve a paper draft. On a dataset of paired submission and camera-ready papers from ICLR and NeurIPS, we compare the helpfulness of questions from GPT models with or without full paper context to that of human reviewers. GPT produces more edit-inducing questions and its questions are associated with more extensive edits and cover a broader range of edited content compared to questions from reviewers. However, a much smaller percentage of the GPT questions are edit-inducing. Our analyses confirm that automated questions can be beneficial to authors and highlight an example task where proper attending to long context deteriorates reasoning model ability to produce helpful output.

</details>


### [169] [Gödel Forest: Balancing Search Depth and Breadth for Data-Centric Recursive Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36675)
*Ziqi Zhao,Fanqing Meng,Haocheng Lu,Lingxiao Du,Qiguang Chen,Mengkang Hu,Xiao-Ming Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出G"odel Forest多智能体框架，通过共演搜索树和动态共享内存解决递归自我改进（RSI）中探索广度与深度的矛盾，在RSIBench-Data上平均提升10.70%并减少耗时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据-centric RSI方法面临两难困境：单智能体缺乏探索广度易陷入局部最优，而并行搜索或重日志共享牺牲了长视界搜索深度，且验证数据策略成本高昂。

Method: 构建G"odel Forest框架，将RSI组织为共演搜索树的集成。每个智能体自主生长持久化树以深化/分支/修剪策略确保深度；并行树探索不同数据空间区域以扩展广度。关键创新在于引入动态共演记忆，通过全局排行榜将成功与失败蒸馏为紧凑的程序性教训，连接各树以实现集体知识蒸馏。

Result: 在RSIBench-Data的六个多样化领域评估中，G"odel Forest相比单智能体基线平均性能提升10.70%，并在五个任务上减少了墙钟时间。消融实验表明，共演共享内存相比独立并行搜索带来+7.00%的性能增益，证实集体蒸馏对可扩展自我改进的关键作用。

Conclusion: 通过森林生态系统机制，单一路径的死胡同可即时警示全森林避开不利路径，而实证突破能迅速在邻近树中播种新探索分支。该方法证明了结合深度搜索、广度探索与集体知识蒸馏是实现高效、可扩展递归自我改进的有效途径。

Abstract: Recursive self-improvement (RSI) aims to achieve compounding gains by having models improve themselves. While most existing RSI systems optimize external agent harnesses or prompts around a frozen base model, data-centric RSI directly updates the model's own parameters by training on agent-generated data. However, because validating data strategies requires expensive model training, existing methods face a fundamental dilemma: a single agent gets trapped in narrow directions and lacks exploration breadth, while naive parallel search or heavy trace sharing sacrifices long-horizon search depth. To address this challenge, we introduce G"odel Forest, a multi-agent framework that organizes recursive self-improvement as an ensemble of co-evolving search trees. In G"odel Forest, each agent autonomously grows a persistent tree, deepening, branching, or pruning data strategies based on model feedback to secure depth, while parallel trees explore distinct regions of the data space to expand breadth. Crucially, rather than leaving trees isolated or flooding them with heavy execution logs, a dynamically co-evolving memory connects the forest: agents continuously distill their successes and failures into compact procedural lessons anchored to a global leaderboard. Through this forest ecosystem, a dead-end in one tree instantly warns the whole forest against unpromising paths, while an empirical breakthrough quickly seeds new exploration branches in neighboring trees. Evaluated on RSIBench-Data across six diverse domains, G"odel Forest outperforms the single-agent baseline by an average of 10.70% while reducing wall-clock time on five tasks. Ablations confirm that co-evolving shared memory yields a +7.00% gain over independent parallel search, demonstrating that collective distillation is key to scalable self-improvement. The code is available at https://github.com/evolvent-ai/Godel-Forest.

</details>


### [170] [ProgressCompass: Embodied Progress Reward Models Are Lost Without the Right Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36684)
*Jianshu Zhang,Keliang Wu,Chengxuan Qian,Xiyuan Yang,Ce Zhang,Ariel Tian,Anbang Liu,Haoran Lu,Han Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对具身智能体在长任务中因缺乏历史上下文导致进度评估不准的问题，提出ContextProgress-Bench基准和ProgressCompass方法。通过通用视觉语言模型（VLM）为预训练进度奖励模型（PRM）补充必要上下文，显著降低进度误差并提升评估准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有进度奖励模型（PRMs）在长任务中面临“上下文依赖的进度估计”难题，即仅凭当前帧无法判断任务进展，因为进度往往依赖于历史信息。现有基准多关注短任务，缺乏对需要上下文推理场景的探索。

Method: 1. 构建ContextProgress-Bench基准：包含24个操作任务、120个episode，涵盖状态回忆、序列追踪和重复消除歧义三种设置。
2. 进行配对诊断：验证集成正确上下文的指令能显著提升PRM性能。
3. 提出ProgressCompass：一种自主智能体循环机制，利用通用VLM为冻结的PRM提供其所需的上下文信息，以重新定向PRM的评估能力。

Result: 1. 诊断显示，即使读取完整历史的PRM在没有正确上下文时也会迷失，但加入正确上下文后，5个模型的进度误差减少了77-82%。
2. ProgressCompass使相同的冻结PRM进度误差降低了63%，等级一致性提高了76%。
3. 该方法显著提升了PRM在更长、更复杂任务上的进度估计能力。

Conclusion: 具身PRM并非不能进行进度估计，而是缺乏必要的上下文。通过ProgressCompass引入通用VLM作为上下文提供者，可以有效解决上下文依赖问题，大幅提升长任务中的进度监控与评估效果。

Abstract: Embodied agents now take on ever longer tasks. For long tasks, knowing only whether a task finally succeeds or fails says little; the steps along the way matter. Progress Reward Models (PRMs) score how far a task has come at every step, and serve as dense rewards, verifiers and monitors. Yet in long tasks the current frame alone often cannot tell how far the task has come, because progress depends on what happened before. We call this problem context-dependent progress estimation. Existing benchmarks on progress estimation mostly focus on short tasks whose progress can be read from the current observation, and whether PRMs can estimate progress when context is needed remains underexplored. We therefore build ContextProgress-Bench, with 24 manipulation tasks for 120 episodes. The benchmark covers three settings: (i) State Recall, where information needed for progress appeared earlier but is not in the current frame; (ii) Sequence Tracking, where steps follow a fixed order, so progress requires knowing which steps are done and which comes next; and (iii) Recurrence Disambiguation, where look-alike frames sit at very different progress. We then run a paired diagnosis: each PRM keeps the same input format in both runs, and in one run its instruction integrates the right context. Even PRMs that read the entire history get lost in estimating progress, yet with the right context the same five models cut their progress error by 77-82%. Embodied PRMs are thus not incapable of progress estimation, but lost without the right context. We therefore propose ProgressCompass, an autonomous agentic loop that reorients an existing PRM and uses current general-purpose VLMs to supply the context the PRM needs. Wrapped in the loop, the same frozen PRM cuts its progress error by 63% and raises its rank agreement by 76%. With such a compass, PRMs estimate progress far better on longer, more complex tasks.

</details>


### [171] [Video2Skill: From Streaming Experience to Reusable Embodied Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36691)
*Jianshu Zhang,Ce Zhang,Xiyuan Yang,Chenwei Xu,Haoran Lu,Yijiang Li,Yaqi Xie,Katia P. Sycara,Han Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究探讨了视觉语言模型（VLMs）能否将连续的视频事件流转化为可复用的技能，提出了Streaming Embodied Skill Discovery (SESD) 问题和Video2Skill基准测试。研究发现现有VLMs在技能分组上表现接近随机水平，且存在“熟悉技能过度巩固、新技能扩展不足”的瓶颈，核心挑战在于判断何时需要创建新技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能体通过复用少量基础技能来泛化到新任务中，但前提是该智能体必须“知道”这些技能。从观察经验中恢复技能（规划的对偶问题）对于构建智能体的知识体系及训练未来智能体至关重要。尽管VLMs能很好地描述单个操作事件，但其是否能将一系列事件组织成可复用的技能尚不明确。

Method: 1. 提出Streaming Embodied Skill Discovery (SESD) 框架：模型按顺序观看视频并维护一个持久的技能库以指导后续决策。
2. 引入Video2Skill基准测试：涵盖机器人桌面操作和人类厨房活动，评估三项核心能力：(i) 时间定位操作事件；(ii) 将相同变换的事件分组；(iii) 决定复用现有技能或创建新技能。
3. 测试了19个开源VLMs，并采用监督微调方法，包括提出的反事实库状态重平衡（CLaRe）。

Result: 1. 多数VLMs在事件分组上的表现接近随机水平，且模型规模扩大并未带来一致的性能提升。
2. 错误类型取决于感知与库更新的耦合方式：联合模型倾向于将不同的变换合并为一个技能，而基于文本描述更新库的模型则倾向于重复已有技能。
3. 监督微调（含CLaRe）虽改善了分组能力，但暴露出深层瓶颈：模型仅巩固熟悉技能，极少扩展新技能，导致技能库大小停滞在参考值的一半以下。
4. 训练集中未见过的变换虽能被时间定位，但几乎从未被赋予新技能。

Conclusion: 当前VLMs在从视频流中发现和复用技能方面存在显著局限。主要瓶颈不在于识别具体操作，而在于“认知缺口”——即模型难以判断现有技能是否不足以完成任务，从而缺乏创建新技能的主动性。如何使模型准确识别现有技能的局限性并据此扩展技能库，是未来的核心挑战。

Abstract: Manipulation behaviors vary widely across objects and scenes, but they share a small set of reusable skills, and planning with these skills helps embodied agents generalize to new tasks. Yet an agent can only plan with skills it knows. Recovering skills from observed experience, the inverse of planning, builds this knowledge over time and yields skill data for training future agents. Vision-Language Models (VLMs) describe individual manipulation events well, but can they organize a stream of events into reusable skills? We formulate this problem as Streaming Embodied Skill Discovery (SESD): a model watches videos in sequence and maintains a persistent skill library that shapes its later decisions. To systematically measure this ability, we introduce Video2Skill, a benchmark that covers robot tabletop manipulation and human kitchen activity and tests three core capabilities: (i) locating manipulation events in time, (ii) grouping events of the same transformation, and (iii) deciding when to reuse an existing skill or create a new one. Across 19 open-source VLMs, many models group events at near-chance level, and scale does not consistently help. Their errors depend on how perception and library updates are coupled: joint models merge distinct transformations into one skill, while models that update the library from text descriptions duplicate recurring ones. Supervised fine-tuning, including our counterfactual library-state rebalancing (CLaRe), improves grouping but exposes a deeper bottleneck: trained models consolidate familiar skills yet rarely expand the library. Their libraries stall below half the reference size, and transformations unseen in training are located in time but almost never given a new skill. Recognizing when existing skills are insufficient thus emerges as the central challenge.

</details>


### [172] [Lost in Conversation or Lost in Translation? Diagnosing Multi-Turn Degradation in RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36700)
*Pranav Handa,Ariful Azad*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究揭示了多轮对话场景下RAG系统性能显著下降的问题，发现相比单轮查询，多轮交互导致准确率降低高达21%，可靠性降低47%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的检索增强生成（RAG）及其图变体GraphRAG主要在单轮、完整指定的查询上进行评估，这与用户在实际与大语言模型（LLM）交互时通过多轮跟进构建复杂问题的真实场景存在严重的不匹配。

Method: 通过大规模模拟研究，将多跳问答基准中的问题转化为不完整的对话场景，评估了十个LLM助手与八个检索系统在150万次模拟对话中的表现。

Result: 多轮交互导致广泛的性能退化：相对性能下降最高达21%，不可靠性增加47%。识别出两种主要失效模式：“迷失在翻译中”（对话重述扭曲了检索查询）和“迷失在对话中”（检索成功但LLM未能跨轮次综合证据）。

Conclusion: 当前的RAG系统在多轮对话环境中同时面临准确性和可靠性的挑战，现有评估方法未能反映实际使用中的性能瓶颈。

Abstract: When conversing with large language models (LLMs), users often begin with a simple question and build towards a multi-hop question through follow-up turns. Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) and its graph-based variant (GraphRAG) have become the dominant approaches for grounding LLM responses in external evidence, yet both are evaluated almost exclusively on single-turn, fully specified queries. We systematically investigate this evaluation mismatch through a large-scale simulation study. Building on prior work on multi-turn LLM evaluation, we transform questions from multi-hop question answering (QA) benchmarks into underspecified conversations and evaluate ten LLM assistants with eight retrieval systems across 1.5 million simulated conversations. Our findings reveal that multi-turn interaction causes widespread performance degradation, incurring relative performance drops of up to 21% and increasing unreliability by 47%, making RAG systems simultaneously less accurate and less reliable. We identify two distinct failure modes behind this degradation.
